### [168] [Sphere path integrals for fermionic gauge fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19275)
*Chiara Baracco,Vasileios A. Letsios*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维球面 $S^4$（dS$_4$ 的欧几里得延拓）上自旋 $s\geq 3/2$ 的复严格无质量费米规范场，计算一圈球面路径积分并用 bulk 与 edge 特征标表示，给出对数发散系数，并证实无质量费米子塔的精确一圈相消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 $s=3/2$ 已有结果，需要推广到更高自旋费米规范场；同时需厘清 dS$_4$ 上费米规范场量子化的不同方案，并检验其与玻色子情形在一圈层次上的差异（如虚相位问题），以及验证近期发现的费米子塔一圈相消现象。

Method: 在 $S^4$ 上对 $s=5/2$ 讨论量子化方案后，计算一圈球面路径积分并表达为泛函行列式，推广 $s=3/2$ 的做法；将结果改写为 de Sitter 等距群 $Spin(4,1)$ 离散序列中的 bulk 酉 Harish-Chandra 特征标与 edge 特征标；再把特征标表示推广到任意复严格无质量自旋 $s\geq 3/2$ 的费米规范势；并计算所有自旋的对数发散系数。

Result: 得到任意 $s\geq 3/2$ 复严格无质量费米规范势的一圈 $S^4$ 路径积分特征标表达式；对数发散系数完全由 bulk 与 edge 特征标编码；证明一圈层次严格无质量费米规范势不出现虚相位（与玻色子相反）；并确认自旋 $s=1/2,3/2,\dots$ 无限复无质量费米子塔的 bulk 与 edge 贡献在一圈精确相消。

Conclusion: 高自旋费米规范场的一圈球面路径积分可统一由 bulk 与 edge 特征标刻画，其发散结构亦被特征标完全编码；费米情形无虚相位，且费米子塔存在精确的一圈 bulk–edge 相消。

Abstract: We consider spin-$s \geq \frac{3}{2}$ complex, strictly massless fermionic gauge fields on the four-sphere, $S^4$, which is the Euclidean continuation of de Sitter spacetime, dS$_4$. For $s=\frac{5}{2}$, after briefly discussing different options for the quantisation of the theory on dS$_4$, we compute the one-loop sphere path integral. We explain how it can be expressed in terms of functional determinants, generalising previous results for $s=\frac{3}{2}$. We proceed to rewrite the result in terms of `bulk' unitary Harish-Chandra characters in the discrete series of the de Sitter isometry group, $Spin(4,1)$, and `edge' characters. We then generalise the character expression of the sphere path integral for any complex, strictly massless spin-$s\geq\frac{3}{2}$ fermionic gauge potential. Additionally, we compute the coefficient of the logarithmic divergence of the $S^4$ path integral for all spin-$s \geq \frac{3}{2}$, and we show that it is fully encoded by bulk and edge characters. We further show that at one loop no imaginary phase appears for strictly massless fermionic gauge potentials, contrary to the case of bosons. Lastly, we confirm the exact one-loop cancellation between bulk and edge contributions for the case of an infinite tower of complex massless fermions of spins $s=\frac{1}{2}, \frac{3}{2},\dots$, observed recently.

</details>


### [169] [Learning the Emergent Bulk Geometry of the Three Dimensional Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20210)
*Ritam Basu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用3D临界Ising模型的蒙特卡洛数据与可微AdS₄探针场求解器，反演体时空径向几何，发现单一经典度规无法同时拟合σ与ε两个边界算子的两点函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验全息对偶中"经典体几何"图像在O(1)中心荷理论（如3D Ising模型）中的适用性：是否存在单一bulk度规能同时重现边界不同算子的关联函数。

Method: 以3D Ising模型临界温度的蒙特卡洛数据为输入，构建渐近AdS₄背景上探针场的可微求解器，将动量空间两点函数（σ与ε）作为拟合目标，分别与联合地训练径向度规；并改变动量窗口与格点尺寸做鲁棒性检验。

Result: 单一度规无法同时拟合两个算子：单独拟合每个通道可使其自身残差改善3–5倍，但会使另一通道恶化达两个数量级；该张力在动量窗口与格点尺寸变化下依然存在。

Conclusion: 数值结果表明，具有O(1)中心荷的理论（如3D Ising模型）其体几何不能被单一经典度规所描述，暗示需要超越经典引力/单度规的全息描述。

Abstract: We use Monte Carlo data for the three-dimensional Ising model at the critical temperature to predict an emergent radial geometry of the bulk using machine learning. Specifically, we employ a differentiable solver for a probe field on an asymptotically $\mathrm{AdS}_4$ background, trained to reproduce the measured momentum-space two-point functions of the boundary operators ($σ$ and $ε$). We find that a single metric cannot simultaneously serve both operators. Fitting each channel separately improves its own residual by a factor of three to five, but degrades the other by up to two orders of magnitude. We quantify this tension between the two operators and show that it survives changes to the momentum window and the lattice size. Our results numerically demonstrate that the bulk geometry of a theory with an $\mathcal{O}(1)$ central charge, such as the 3D Ising model, is not captured by a single classical metric.

</details>


### [170] [Electroweak balls: non-topological solitons in the Weinberg-Salam theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19293)
*Carlos Herdeiro,Jutta Kunz,Burkhard Kleihaus,Eugen Radu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在玻色子 SU(2)×U(1) Weinberg-Salam 理论中构造了一类新的光滑、有限能量孤子，称为"电弱球"（electroweak balls），其定域机制类比于 Q-ball，带电矢量场具谐波时间依赖而能量-动量张量为静态。作者显式构造了球对称电型与轴对称磁型解，并发现解族具有有限频率区间、质量间隙和双支结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将 Q-ball 的定域机制推广至电弱理论框架，寻求 Proca-Higgs ball 的电弱对应物，其中矢量玻色子质量由 Higgs 机制而非人为引入产生，并探索解族对理论参数的依赖关系。

Method: 在玻色子 SU(2)×U(1) Weinberg-Salam 理论中构造孤子解：令带电矢量场具有谐波时间依赖（$e^{-i\omega t}$ 形式）以保持能量-动量张量时不变，从而获得有限能量、光滑的定域构型；分别构建球对称的电型解和轴对称的磁型解。

Result: 得到了电型和磁型两类解族，它们表现出有限频率区间、质量间隙及双支结构；这些构型是 Proca-Higgs ball 的电弱对应物，矢量玻色子质量由 Higgs 机制产生；构造不依赖于标准模型实测参数，可推广至不同规范耦合、Higgs 自耦合和对称破缺标度的电弱型部门。但在实测标准模型耦合与质量比下未找到解。

Conclusion: 电弱球构成一类新的有限能量孤子解族，验证了 Q-ball 式定域机制在电弱理论中的可行性，且其存在性并不限定于标准模型实测参数；然而在真实标准模型参数下未获得解，表明此类孤子可能只存在于参数空间的其他区域。

Abstract: We construct a new class of smooth, finite-energy solitons in the bosonic $SU(2)\times U(1)$ Weinberg-Salam theory, which we dub $electroweak$ $balls$. Their localization mechanism is analogous in spirit to that of $Q$-balls: the charged vector fields possess a harmonic time dependence while the energy-momentum tensor remains time independent. We explicitly construct both spherically symmetric electric-type solutions and axisymmetric magnetic-type solutions, and show that they form families characterized by a finite frequency interval, a mass gap, and a two-branch structure. These configurations provide electroweak counterparts of Proca-Higgs balls, with the vector-boson masses generated by the Higgs mechanism rather than introduced explicitly. The construction is not tied to the measured parameters of the Standard Model. More generally, it applies to bosonic electroweak-type sectors with different gauge couplings, Higgs self-coupling and symmetry-breaking scale, and hence potentially very different characteristic particle and soliton mass scales. For the families studied here, we do not find solutions at the measured Standard Model couplings and mass ratios.

</details>


### [171] [Fortuity on the conifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19297)
*Jaehyeok Choi,Sunjin Choi,Seok Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Klebanov-Witten理论中构建经典上同调问题，以研究典型N=1 AdS5/CFT4模型中的BPS黑洞态，并提出"单调上同调"与"偶然上同调"的细化概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究典型N=1 AdS5/CFT4模型中的BPS黑洞态，需要在强耦合RG不动点（Klebanov-Witten理论）框架下建立可计算的表述。

Method: 在Klebanov-Witten理论中建立经典上同调问题，并在UV弱耦合理论中构建对应的上同调问题，提出单调上同调与偶然上同调的细化概念；在SU(2)×SU(2)理论中构造无穷多偶然上同调。

Result: 在SU(2)×SU(2)理论中构造出无穷多个偶然上同调，并将其中部分解释为N=2版本、由重子凝聚体修饰的"毛状"黑洞态；同时指出近期在ABJM中构造的偶然上同调表现出广义单调性。

Conclusion: 简单重子算符属于单调上同调；偶然上同调可用于描述重子凝聚修饰的黑洞态，且ABJM中的偶然上同调具有广义单调性。

Abstract: To study the BPS black hole states in typical $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$ models, we formulate a classical cohomology problem in the Klebanov-Witten theory, which is defined at a strongly coupled RG fixed point. We set up its weakly coupled cohomology problem in the UV theory, suggesting a refined notion of monotonous and fortuitous cohomologies so that simple baryonic operators belong to the former. We construct infinitely many fortuitous cohomologies in the $SU(2)\times SU(2)$ theory and interpret some of them as $N=2$ versions of hairy black hole states dressed by baryonic condensates. We also comment that the recently constructed fortuitous cohomologies in ABJM exhibit generalized monotonicity.

</details>


### [172] [Current Reconstruction and Higher Interactions in Gauge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19402)
*Carolina Matté Gregory*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视一阶Yang–Mills理论中带独立二形式的Deser流重构，通过固定二次辅助场扇区与一阶规范修正，考察规定曲率相互作用能否在不引入高阶作用量项的情况下加入。结果表明，对紧致半单规范代数，混合Noether恒等式闭合条件强烈约束辅助代表元的选择，且对解析径向类相互作用通常无法避免三阶作用量修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deser式流重构是否允许在规定曲率相互作用下保持不高于二阶的作用量结构，是判断辅助场数据与相互作用是否自洽的关键问题。作者希望厘清二次辅助扇区、一阶规范修正与高阶作用量修正之间的约束关系。

Method: 在含独立二形式的一阶Yang–Mills框架下，固定二次辅助扇区和一阶规范修正；对紧致半单规范代数要求混合Noether恒等式在不含gh作用量项时闭合，以约束代数辅助代表元；针对解析径向类U=Φ(f·f)及首个非线性项c_p(f·f)^p，利用根su(2)子代数内的自由场标度论证；并结合通常协变化与辅助场平移构造显式完成。

Result: Noether恒等式闭合将辅助代表元固定为切向代表元，至多允许每个单理想上的线性曲率与对偶曲率项；当所有二阶作用量系数为零时，对解析径向类无法避免三阶作用量修正，且该结论通过根su(2)子代数的自由场标度论证推广到任意紧致半单规范代数。对p=2，七胶子接触项在实非奇异运动学下抵消非零树级Ward收缩。另在仿射星规范扇区中，严格流反馈与二阶作用量截断选择不同代表元。

Conclusion: 规定的辅助场数据决定了流重构必须要求哪些相互作用：紧致半单情形下辅助代表元受Noether闭合强约束，三阶修正通常不可避免；显式完成与胶子接触项抵消展示了该约束的物理后果。

Abstract: We revisit Deser's current reconstruction in first-order Yang--Mills theory with an independent two-form. We fix the quadratic auxiliary sector and the first gauge correction, and ask whether a prescribed curvature interaction can be added without higher-order action terms. For a compact semisimple gauge algebra, requiring the mixed Noether identity to close without a $gh$ action term fixes the algebraic auxiliary representative to the tangent representative, up to linear curvature and dual-curvature terms on each simple ideal. For the analytic radial class $U=Φ(f\cdot f)$, with first nonlinear term $c_p(f\cdot f)^p$, no allowed representative avoids a third-order action correction when all second-order action coefficients vanish. A free-field scaling argument inside a root $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ subalgebra establishes this result for every compact semisimple gauge algebra. Ordinary covariantization and an auxiliary-field shift give an explicit completion. For $p=2$, its seven-gluon contact term cancels a nonzero tree-level Ward contraction at real nonsingular kinematics. We also study an affine star-gauge sector motivated by Seiberg--Witten maps, where strict current feedback and second-order action truncation select different representatives. Together these results show how the prescribed auxiliary data determine which interactions are required by current reconstruction.

</details>


### [173] [Notes on de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19454)
*Beatrix Mühlmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了具有正宇宙学常数的de Sitter时空的经典与量子特征，涵盖刚性与涨落背景、波函数、黑洞、熵及不同维度的具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正宇宙学常数下de Sitter时空的经典与量子性质是宇宙学与量子引力的重要课题，需要系统梳理其多方面特征与模型。

Method: 综述性论述：先介绍de Sitter宇宙学基本内容与经典几何，继而将de Sitter空间分别视为量子场传播的刚性背景与自身涨落的时空，并回顾膨胀宇宙波函数、de Sitter黑洞、de Sitter熵，比较宇宙视界与黑洞视界。

Result: 系统整理了de Sitter时空的经典与量子特征，比较了宇宙视界与黑洞视界，并给出二、三、四维时空中的具体de Sitter模型。

Conclusion: 最后三节专注于二、三、四维时空中的具体de Sitter空间模型。

Abstract: We discuss classical and quantum features of spacetimes with a positive cosmological constant. After introducing basic aspects of de Sitter cosmology and the classical geometry of de Sitter space, we consider de Sitter space both as a rigid background, on which quantum fields propagate, and as a fluctuating spacetime in its own right. We review the wavefunction of an expanding universe, black holes in de Sitter space, and the de Sitter entropy, and compare the cosmological horizon with the black hole horizon. The final three sections are dedicated to concrete models of de Sitter space in two, three and four spacetime dimensions.

</details>


### [174] [Exploring multi-parameter optimization in FRG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20667)
*A. Codello,G. P. Vacca,D. Zarrilli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出在紧支撑多项式调节子参数空间内，利用最小敏感度原理（PMS）优化函数重整化群（FRG）计算结果，并将其应用于三维Ising普适类的标量场论以最小化反常维数对调节子的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非微扰Wilsonian FRG方程必须在近似方案下求解，导致物理结果依赖于粗粒化调节子的选择；如何在实际计算中选取最优调节子以减小这种人为依赖，是提高FRG预测精度的关键问题。

Method: 在基于Wetterich-Morris方程的1PI表述下，构造由一组变量参数化的紧支撑多项式调节子空间；采用有效平均作用的导数展开至四阶，对描述三维Ising普适类的标量场论进行计算，并以反常维数对调节子的依赖最小化为优化目标实施PMS。

Result: 在四阶导数展开下，PMS优化给出对临界指数的明显修正，相比此前单参数分析改进了预测精度；同时发现若干类紧支撑调节子族，随着参数个数逐步增加，其最优函数形式快速收敛。

Conclusion: 将PMS与多参数紧支撑多项式调节子空间相结合，可系统性地降低FRG结果对调节子的依赖并提升临界指数的预测精度，且该方法具有良好的收敛性，为调节子优化提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The use of nonperturbative Wilsonian functional renormalization group (FRG) equations within an unavoidable approximation scheme produces physical results that depend on the choice of the coarse-graining regulator. This dependence of physical observables on the regulator can be minimized according to the principle of minimal sensitivity (PMS). For the 1PI formulation based on the Wetterich-Morris equation, we propose to investigate the optimal choice within a space of compactly supported polynomial regulators parametrized by a set of variables. We test this approach using the derivative expansion of the effective average action up to fourth order, applying it to the scalar field theory representing the three-dimensional Ising universality class and minimizing the dependence of the anomalous dimension on the regulator. At fourth order, we find appreciable corrections to the critical exponents that improve the predictions, compared to previous one parameter analyses. We also observe families of compact regulators that rapidly converge towards an optimal functional form as a small number of parameters is progressively introduced.

</details>


### [175] [Variational Solution of Non-Hermitian Quantum Field Theory in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19798)
*Paul Romatschke*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出一种求解非厄米量子系统的变分方法，重点处理负四次自耦合情形，并用于d=2格点离散标量场论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米量子系统（尤其含负四次自耦合）难以用标准厄米量子力学的变分技术直接处理，需明确其差异与潜在陷阱。

Method: 将变分方法从标准量子力学推广到非厄米量子力学，指出关键差异与易犯错误，并给出可行的变分实现方案；随后在哈密顿量框架下对d=2格点离散的负四次自耦合标量场论进行变分分析。

Result: 成功实现了非厄米情形的变分方法，并完成了d=2格点离散负四次自耦合标量场论的变分计算与分析。

Conclusion: 变分方法可有效推广至非厄米量子系统；在哈密顿量格点表述下，该技术为研究负四次自耦合标量场论提供了可行途径。

Abstract: This work describes a variational technique to solve non-Hermitian quantum systems, in particular those with negative coupling quartic self-interaction. Differences of applying variational methods from standard quantum mechanics to non-Hermitian quantum mechanics -- including potential pitfalls -- are highlighted. A successful implementation of the variational approach for the non-Hermitian case is presented. The technique is used to analyze scalar quantum field theory in d=2 with negative quartic self-interaction in the Hamiltonian formulation in lattice discretization.

</details>


### [176] [Conformal blocks and braiding matrices for RCFTs I: Virasoro minimal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19904)
*Suresh Govindarajan,Aditya Jain,Akhila Sadanandan,Prasanta K. Tripathy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出系统计算RCFT四点函数编织矩阵的方法，并在Virasoro极小模型中实现，绕过null-state推导，数值计算F矩阵后化为精确结果，并讨论向扩展对称性及全纯模bootsrap的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶null state条件下利用null-state条件推导Fuchsian (BPZ) ODE的accessory参数过程繁琐，需要一种更直接、系统的途径来计算RCFT四点函数的编织（braiding）矩阵与F矩阵。

Method: 通过Shapovalov形式直接计算共形块级数的前几项，确定三阶及以上Fuchsian (BPZ) ODE的accessory参数，从而绕过null-state条件推导；将编织F矩阵视为ODE在两个奇点处Frobenius解之间的连接矩阵，先数值计算并与Dotsenko–Fateev的Coulomb-gas结果对比；再通过基变换使F矩阵幺正化，以此确定三点结构常数（的乘积），并猜想幺正F矩阵元素的平方属于有理数的分圆扩张，从而将数值估计转为精确值。

Result: F矩阵的数值结果与使用Coulomb-gas形式的Dotsenko–Fateev结果一致；经基变换后F矩阵可化为幺正形式，由此确定三点结构常数（乘积）；猜想幺正F矩阵元素的平方位于有理数的分圆扩张中，从而可将数值估计转换为精确值；在Virasoro极小模型中用大量例子进行了说明。

Conclusion: 所提形式化方法可行且有效，可用于计算RCFT四点函数的编织矩阵；方法可推广至扩展Virasoro对称性的情形，以及源于全纯模bootstrap纲领的可处理例子。

Abstract: We develop a systematic method for computing the braiding matrices of four-point functions in RCFTs. This is implemented for the case of Virasoro minimal models. The first few terms of the conformal block series are directly computed through the Shapovalov form. The Fuchsian (BPZ) ODEs for order three and higher have accessory parameters that are fixed by this direct computation. We thus bypass the derivation from the null-state condition which gets tedious for higher-level null states. The braiding F-matrices are connection matrices between the Frobenius solutions at two singular points of the ODE. The F-matrices are computed numerically at first, agreeing with the results of Dotsenko--Fateev, which uses the Coulomb-gas formalism. We find that after a change of basis, the F-matrix is rendered unitary, which determines (products of) the three-point structure constants. We conjecture that the squares of the entries of the unitary F-matrix lie in a cyclotomic extension of the rational numbers. This enables us to convert our numerical estimates for the F-matrix into exact ones. The formalism is illustrated through numerous examples in Virasoro minimal models. We also discuss how these methods can be extended to cases involving symmetries that extend the Virasoro symmetry as well as for tenable examples that arise from the holomorphic modular bootstrap program.

</details>


### [177] [Nonperturbative Chiral Anomaly Cancellation and Irrelevant Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19917)
*Michele Bianchessi,Vieri Mastropietro,Marcello Porta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对多味Sommerfield模型（Dirac费米子与紧致性缺失的U(1)量子矢量场耦合）的格点Kogut-Susskind正则化变体，给出了手征反常消除及其普适性的严格非微扰证明，并揭示了一种基于重整化展开收敛性的新普适机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征反常消除在微扰论中已充分理解，但在格点存在下的非微扰有效性尚未被证明；本文旨在填补这一非微扰证明的空白，并阐明其普适性来源。

Method: 在多味Sommerfield模型的格点Kogut-Susskind正则化变体框架下，结合重整化展开的收敛性质进行严格非微扰分析；识别出与拓扑绝缘体普适性相关的、新的普适机制，并考察无关格点算符的贡献。

Result: 严格证明格点上手征反常消除及其普适性成立；发现无关格点算符沿重整化群流动虽被压低，却对反常消除提供不可或缺的贡献。

Conclusion: 格点手征反常消除可由重整化展开收敛性所驱动的普适机制保证，无关算符在其中起关键作用，表明格点非微扰层面反常消除的普适性具有坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The Chiral anomaly cancellation is perturbatively well understood but its nonperturbative validity in presence of a lattice has remained unproven. We provide a rigorous nonperturbative proof of anomaly cancellation and its universality in a variation of the lattice Kogut-Susskind regularization for the multiflavor Sommerfield model, describing Dirac fermions interacting with a non-compact $\mathrm{U}(1)$ quantum vector field. We identify a novel and general mechanism based on the convergence properties of the renormalized expansion and related to the one leading to universality in topological insulators. Irrelevant lattice operators, despite being suppressed along the RG flow, provide essential contributions to the anomaly cancellation.

</details>


### [178] [Explicit construction of the energy-momentum tensor in the large N limit of a four-Fermi theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20054)
*Carlo Pagani,Hidenori Sonoda*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用精确重整化群（泛函RG）形式，在大N极限下构造了2<D<4欧几里得空间中四费米子理论的能量-动量张量，并证明其满足平移与转动Ward-Takahashi恒等式，同时通过相应恒等式推导出临界理论具有共形不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非整数维（2<D<4）的四费米子理论中，如何系统地定义一个紫外完备且满足对称性要求的能量-动量张量，以及临界点是否具有共形不变性，是需要借助精确重整化群框架加以澄清的问题。

Method: 采用精确重整化群（泛函RG）形式，在大N极限下将能量-动量张量构造为Dirac场的泛函，其截断依赖性由精确RG方程控制；在零截断极限下该泛函退化为单次插入能量-动量张量的一粒子不可约有效作用量，并在有限截断下检验平移不变性的Ward-Takahashi恒等式与转动不变性对反对称部分的确定。

Result: 所构造的能量-动量张量在有限截断下满足平移不变性的Ward-Takahashi恒等式，其反对称部分由转动不变性确定；通过推导相应的Ward-Takahashi恒等式，证明临界理论是共形不变的。

Conclusion: 精确RG方法为在2<D<4维四费米子理论中构造受对称性约束的能量-动量张量提供了系统途径，并揭示了其临界理论具有共形不变性。

Abstract: Using the exact renormalization group (functional RG) formalism, we
  construct the energy-momentum tensor of a four-Fermi theory in the
  Euclidean space of dimensions 2 < D < 4 in the large N limit,
  where N is the number of Dirac fields. The energy-momentum tensor
  is a functional of the Dirac fields, and its cutoff dependence is
  controlled by the exact RG equation. In the zero cutoff limit the
  functional reduces to the one-particle-irreducible effective action
  with a single insertion of the energy-momentum tensor. At a finite
  cutoff the energy-momentum tensor satisfies the Ward-Takahashi
  identity for translation invariance, and its antisymmetric part is
  determined by rotation invariance. We also show that the critical
  theory is conformally invariant by deriving the corresponding
  Ward-Takahashi identity.

</details>


### [179] [Supersymmetric boundary algebras in AdS$_3$ gravity: constraints, Dirac brackets and non-locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20112)
*Nabamita Banerjee,Vedant Bhutra,Suvankar Dutta,Soumava Kundu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过将边界条件视为Chern-Simons边界相空间的约束约化，研究AdS₃引力中的超对称边界动力学，重点分析约束结构如何决定约化渐近对称代数的定域性与物理场内容，并用BFV形式化消除非定域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdS₃引力的边界动力学通常由Chern-Simons理论描述，但不同边界条件会通过约束结构影响约化后渐近对称代数的定域性和物理自由度。作者希望系统理解约束结构（特别是超对称情形下的费米子约束）如何控制这些性质，并解决约化对称代数中出现的非定域性问题。

Method: 将边界条件处理为一般Chern-Simons边界相空间的约束约化；分析超对称Kac-Moody边界条件下Dirac括号的定域性；施加额外费米子约束研究次级约束的第一类/第二类性质及规范冗余；最后采用Batalin-Fradkin-Tyutin（BFV）形式化，将第二类约束转换为扩大相空间上等价的定域第一类系统。

Result: 对超对称Kac-Moody边界条件，残余玻色流保持定域，而费米子-费米子Dirac括号包含场依赖的一阶微分算符之逆，因而本质非定域。施加额外费米子约束后，在玻色第二类约化之后它变为第一类并生成费米规范冗余；通常剩余费米场可被规范消除，但具有周期性零模的特殊背景支持非平凡物理费米扇区。BFV形式化成功将非定域约化对称代数转化为扩大相空间上的定域第一类系统。

Conclusion: 约束结构是控制AdS₃超对称边界理论中对称代数定域性和物理场内容的关键。费米子扇区可表现出本质非定域性，并可通过约束的第一类/第二类转变产生规范冗余或非平凡物理模。BFV方法为消除非定域性、构造等价定域第一类描述提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We study supersymmetric boundary dynamics in AdS$_3$ gravity by treating boundary conditions as constrained reductions of a generic Chern-Simons boundary phase space. We focus on how the constraint structure controls the locality and physical field content of the reduced asymptotic symmetry algebra. For supersymmetric Kac--Moody boundary conditions, the surviving bosonic currents remain local, whereas the fermion--fermion Dirac bracket contains the inverse of a field-dependent first-order differential operator and is therefore intrinsically non-local. Imposing an additional fermionic constraint reveals a further structure: after the bosonic second-class reduction, it becomes first class and generates a fermionic gauge redundancy. Generically the remaining fermionic field can be gauged away, while special backgrounds with periodic zero modes support non-trivial physical fermionic sectors. We further employ the Batalin--Fradkin--Tyutin formalism to eliminate the non-locality of the reduced symmetry algebra by converting the second-class constraints into an equivalent local first-class system on an enlarged phase space.

</details>


### [180] [Dirac oscillator in a self-gravitating cosmic string: confinement spectra beyond the conical approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20164)
*Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将横向Dirac振子束缚态谱作为探针，检验有限芯（finite-core）宇宙弦引力场在理想锥近似之外的可分辨结构，发现束缚态谱携带渐近锥无法确定的芯剖面信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理宇宙弦的引力场在轴上正则、跨有限涡旋芯弯曲，仅在渐近区呈锥形，而通常的ideal-cone近似忽略了有限芯结构。作者希望检验横向Dirac振子的束缚态谱能否分辨这一结构。

Method: 将费米子视为自洽Einstein–Abelian–Higgs涡旋背景上的试探场，把径向问题写成广义Hermitian本征值问题；数值方案用精确Minkowski与ideal-cone谱以及独立的打靶计算进行校验；并对参考涡旋族考察有限芯修正。

Result: 有限芯修正从弱束缚区向百分之一量级增长；在固定归一化渐近锥时，谱仍随测得的Higgs芯半径变化，所采样背景甚至出现基态修正变号。

Conclusion: 束缚态谱包含渐近锥无法单独固定的、关于分辨芯剖面的信息，说明Dirac振子谱可作为探测宇宙弦有限芯结构的有效手段。

Abstract: The gravitational field of a physical cosmic string is regular on the axis, curved across a finite vortex core, and only asymptotically conical. We test whether the bound-state spectrum of a transverse Dirac oscillator resolves this structure beyond the ideal-cone approximation. The fermion is treated as a test field on self-consistent Einstein--Abelian-Higgs vortex backgrounds, and the radial problem is cast as a generalized Hermitian eigenvalue problem. The numerical formulation is checked against the exact Minkowski and ideal-cone spectra and by independent shooting calculations. For a reference vortex family, the finite-core correction grows from the weak-confinement regime toward the percent level. When the normalized asymptotic cone is held fixed, the spectrum still varies with the measured Higgs-core radius, and the sampled backgrounds bracket a change of sign of the ground-state correction. The bound-state spectrum therefore contains information about the resolved core profiles that is not fixed by the asymptotic cone alone.

</details>


### [181] [The Page Transition in the Multi-Time Correlations of Hawking Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20456)
*Tushar Waghmare*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究Page曲线与island公式在Hawking辐射中的信息重组，如何在多个延迟时刻收集的辐射关联中体现。作者将晚期辐射建模为协变多时间过程（process tensor），用χ_island度量早期辐射寄存器R被提供时所移除的时间记忆，并揭示Page转变可表现为辐射过程马尔可夫阶的转变。通过Hayden–Preskill蒸发梳与蒸发JT计算验证该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Page曲线与island公式描述了Hawking辐射在Page时间前后的量子信息重组，但这种重组如何在多个延迟时刻收集的辐射关联（即多时间统计）中具体显现尚不清楚。作者希望从操作层面刻画这一重组，并给出可量化的时间记忆指标。

Method: 将晚期辐射视为协变多时间过程（process tensor），定义χ_island = N_∅ − N_R，用以度量当早期辐射寄存器R被提供时被移除的时间记忆；操作上，该量等于辐射过程相对马尔可夫参考的最优可区分度的变化。构造单bin态及低于有限阶k_*≥3的所有过程边缘分布保持固定、且与CP-divisible参考一致的蒸发过程，考察χ_island在k_*阶发生量级为1的变化。利用纠缠楔重构从R预测性内部，实现晚期过程的马尔可夫化；并形式化R的马尔可夫化如何作为其影响的模拟器。以Hayden–Preskill蒸发梳与蒸发JT模型具体实现。

Result: 存在蒸发过程，其单bin态与所有低于某有限阶k_*≥3的过程边缘分布保持不变并与CP-divisible参考重合，而χ_island在k_*阶改变量级为1。因此Page转变可表现为辐射过程马尔可夫阶的转变。由内部中介的晚期时间记忆，经纠缠楔从R重构预测性内部后使晚期过程马尔可夫化；反之，当晚期过程具有最小预测记忆且其影响未来的信息由该记忆中介时，R的马尔可夫化提供了其影响的模拟器。Hayden–Preskill蒸发梳与蒸发JT计算均实现了该机制。

Conclusion: 晚期辐射在Page转变前后保留时间记忆，而早期辐射变得足以预测其未来的多时间统计。Page转变的实质可被理解为辐射过程马尔可夫阶的转变，χ_island刻画了由R供给所移除的时间记忆，纠缠楔重构与马尔可夫化提供了操作性的模拟与预测方案。

Abstract: The Page curve and the island formula describe a reorganization of quantum information in Hawking radiation across the Page time. We study how this reorganization appears in correlations among emissions collected at several retarded times. The late radiation is treated as a covariant multi-time process, or process tensor, and $χ_{\mathrm{island}}=\mathcal{N}_{\varnothing}-\mathcal{N}_{R}$ measures the temporal memory removed when the early radiation register $R$ is supplied. Operationally, this quantity is the corresponding change in the optimal distinguishability of the radiation process from a Markovian reference. We exhibit evaporation processes whose single-bin states and all process marginals below a finite order $k_\ast\geq 3$ remain fixed and coincide with a CP-divisible reference, while $χ_{\mathrm{island}}$ changes by order one at order $k_\ast$. The Page transition can therefore appear as a transition in the Markov order of the radiation process. When the interior mediates the late-time memory, entanglement-wedge reconstruction of the predictive interior from $R$ Markovianizes the late process; conversely, when the late process has a minimal predictive memory and its future-affecting information is mediated by that memory, Markovianization by $R$ supplies a simulator of its influence. A Hayden--Preskill evaporation comb and an evaporating-JT calculation realize this mechanism. The late radiation retains temporal memory across the transition, while the early radiation becomes sufficient for predicting its future multi-time statistics.

</details>


### [182] [Four-Point Yang-Mills Correlators in AdS$_4$ from All-Line Recursion: All-Plus, Single-Minus and MHV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20493)
*Dhruv Pathak*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文回顾了 AdS₄ 中 Yang-Mills 理论的全线递推关系，计算了 All-plus、Single-minus 与 MHV 螺旋度构型的四点关联函数，并给出各留数的显式表达式及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdS 空间中四点关联函数的计算通常依赖 Witten 图等繁琐方法，而 Raju 的全线递推关系提供了一种更高效的替代途径，本文旨在系统地将其应用于不同螺旋度构型并加以验证。

Method: 采用 AdS₄ 中 Yang-Mills 理论的全线递推关系，指认获得关联函数所需的动量腿形变，并显式推导 All-plus、Single-minus 与 MHV 三种构型中所有贡献留数的表达式；随后通过与 Witten 图计算结果进行数值对比来检验。

Result: 显式给出了三种螺旋度构型四点关联函数对应的全部留数表达式；数值检验结果与 Witten 图计算完全一致。

Conclusion: 该递推方法对 AdS₄ 中 Yang-Mills 四点关联函数是有效且正确的，可进一步推广用于求解更高点数的关联函数。

Abstract: We review Raju's all-line recursion relations for Yang-Mills theory in AdS${}_4$ and evaluate four-point correlators for the All-plus, Single-minus, and MHV helicity configurations. We point out the relevant deformation for the legs needed to obtain the correlators. In particular, we explicitly derive the expressions for all the residues contributing to all three correlators. We also perform an explicit numerical check with the results computed by Witten diagram computations and find perfect agreement with our results. Finally, we conclude by discussing some applications of these recursions for obtaining higher-point functions.

</details>


### [183] [Holographic correlators with non-supersymmetric multi-particle states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20534)
*Michele Giorgi,Stefano Giusto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究HHLL全息关联函数，其中重态为n粒子算符的相干叠加。在大n、大中心荷c且n/c固定的极限下，这些关联函数由经典超引力计算描述，并推测即使多粒子算符非超对称，n/c→0极限仍编码树级连通四点贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知当多粒子算符为半BPS时，HHLL关联函数的n/c→0极限可编码重算符被两个有限n粒子算符替换的四点函数树级连通贡献。作者希望检验该关系在非超对称情形下是否依然成立，从而推广其适用范围。

Method: 在HHLL全息关联函数框架下，将重态取为n粒子算符的相干叠加；取大n、大中心荷c且n/c固定的极限，采用经典超引力计算；并在AdS₃/CFT₂背景下进行验证，考察n/c→0极限的行为。

Result: 研究提供了证据表明，即使多粒子算符非超对称，n/c→0极限的HHLL关联函数仍编码相应的树级连通四点贡献。验证在AdS₃/CFT₂设置中完成。

Conclusion: 该关系并非半BPS情形的特例，而可能在非超对称情形下同样成立；作者据此推测该猜想可推广至更一般的全息对偶。

Abstract: We consider heavy-heavy-light-light (HHLL) holographic correlators in which the heavy states are coherent superpositions of $n$-particle operators. In the limit of large $n$ and large central charge, $c$, with $n/c$ held fixed, these HHLL correlators are described by a classical supergravity computation. When the multi-particle operators are half-BPS, it has been shown that the $n/c\to 0$ limit of the HHLL correlator encodes the tree-level connected contribution to the 4-point function with the heavy operators replaced by two $n$-particle operators with finite $n$. We provide evidence that this relation continues to hold when the multi-particle operators are non-supersymmetric. Although our checks are carried out in the AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ setting, we expect that our conjecture extends to more general holographic dualities.

</details>


### [184] [New Bethe vacua for $\mathcal{N}=2$ elliptic models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20631)
*Antonio Amariti,Pietro Glorioso,Chiara Mascherpa,Andrea Zanetti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Bethe Ansatz方法研究四维N=2 SU(N)椭圆模型的超共形指标，发现了新的后代解，并将其组织为S-对偶群轨道，推广了N=4 SU(N) SYM的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作在N=4 SU(N) SYM中猜想Bethe Ansatz方程的离散解与其N=1*质量形变的大质量真空之间存在联系。本文旨在将这一关系推广到更一般的N=2 SU(N)椭圆模型，并探索是否存在新的解及其对指标的贡献。

Method: 将超共形指标表示为满足一组超越方程的真空之和，从已知的离散解（称为progenitors）出发构造新的后代解，并将这些解按S-对偶群轨道进行组织，类比其在N=1*形变的椭圆自旋Calogero-Moser模型大质量真空上的作用。

Result: 发现了大量新的后代解，证明这些解可按S-对偶群轨道组织，并确定了新后代解对指标的贡献。

Conclusion: Bethe Ansatz离散解与N=1*形变大质量真空之间的对应关系可推广至N=2 SU(N)椭圆模型，且新的后代解对超共形指标有可计算的贡献。

Abstract: We study the superconformal index of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ elliptic models through the Bethe Ansatz approach, where the index is evaluated as a sum of vacua arising from a set of transcendental equations. Starting from the known discrete solutions of these equations, that we refer to as progenitors, we show that there exist large classes of new descendant solutions. We then show that the solutions can be organized into orbits of the S-duality group, mimicking its action on the massive vacua of the $\mathcal{N}=1^*$ deformations of the elliptic spin Calogero-Moser models. In this way we generalize the results already obtained for $\mathcal{N}=4$ $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ SYM, where a relation between the discrete solutions to the Bethe Ansatz Equations and the massive vacua of its $\mathcal{N}=1^*$ massive deformation was conjectured. We conclude our analysis by determining the contributions of the new descendant solutions to the index.

</details>


### [185] [Young Gerard storming high energy physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20695)
*John Iliopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 作者以个人回忆形式，记述了1972年1月在巴黎大学Orsay校区举行的一次科学会议，会上Gerard 't Hooft首次详细报告了其在Yang-Mills理论重整化性质方面的开创性工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借Gerard 't Hooft八十寿辰之机，回顾并记录一个具有历史意义的学术会议场景，保存关于Yang-Mills理论重整化这一里程碑成果首次公开详细报告的第一手记忆。

Method: 采用个人回忆（recollections）的叙述方式，对1972年1月会议的时间、地点及相关学术内容进行回顾性描述，属于科学史性质的历史记述，而非实验或理论计算研究。

Result: 文中指出该会议于1972年1月在巴黎大学Orsay校区举行，Gerard 't Hooft在会上首次详尽阐述了他的开创性工作。

Conclusion: 该文强调1972年这次会议是't Hooft关于Yang-Mills理论重整化性质（涵盖无质量相与自发对称破缺相）的开创性成果首次被详细报告的历史节点。

Abstract: On the occasion of Gerard 't Hooft's 80th birthday, I present my personal recollections of a scientific meeting held in the Orsay Campus of the University of Paris in January 1972. In this meeting Gerard gave for the first time a detailed account of his groundbreaking work on the renormalization properties of Yang-Mills theories, both in the massless phase as well as in the phase with spontaneous symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [186] [Toward a Unique Filter for the Gravitational Path Integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20697)
*Marc S. Klinger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 从代数视角考察 McNamara-Wang 构造，用条件期望理论验证 GPI 存在唯一酉完备化的猜想，并将可约 QFT 的对称性刻画为弱 Hopf 代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McNamara 和 Wang 此前证明引力路径积分（GPI）的因子化失效可理解为态谱不完备，方法是构造一个 GPI 可嵌入的酉量子场论（QFT）。本文希望从代数的角度重新审视这一构造，并检验作者关于 GPI 存在且唯一的酉完备化的猜想。

Method: 采用代数方法，具体运用条件期望（conditional expectation）理论来分析 GPI 与其酉完备化之间的关系；将连接两个理论的条件期望解释为 Liu 意义下的"滤波器"；并借助弱 Hopf 代数对称性刻画可约 QFT 的结构。

Result: 验证了作者关于 GPI 酉完备化存在性与唯一性的猜想；说明酉 QFT 可视为单一自洽理论，其中系综平均的定量特征纯粹来自向底层光滑 GPI 的投影；阐明了可约 QFT 的结构一般由弱 Hopf 代数对称性编码，且弱结构允许广义 α-扇区与半虫洞共存于同一希尔伯特空间中。

Conclusion: GPI 因子化失效对应谱不完备，可通过条件期望实现唯一酉完备化；该完备化理论是可约的（空集对应的希尔伯特空间非平凡），其结构由弱 Hopf 代数对称性刻画，并统一容纳广义 α-扇区与半虫洞。

Abstract: In recent work, McNamara and Wang demonstrated that the failure of factorization for a gravitational path integral (GPI) can be understood as an incompleteness of its spectrum of states by constructing a unitary quantum field theory (QFT) into which the GPI embeds. We examine this construction from an algebraic point of view, and use it to verify a conjecture of the author concerning the existence and uniqueness of a unitary completion for the GPI by means of the theory of conditional expectations. We explore how the unitary QFT can be regarded as a single coherent theory in which the quantitative signatures of ensemble averaging emerge purely from projecting to the underlying, smooth GPI. The conditional expectation relating the two theories may therefore be interpreted as a filter in the sense of Liu. The factorizing theory differs from a more standard QFT in the sense that it is reducible, meaning the Hilbert space associated with the empty set is non-trivial. We explain how the structure of reducible QFTs are generically encoded in weak Hopf algebraic symmetries and demonstrate how the weak structure allows for generalized $α$-sectors and half wormholes to coexist within a single Hilbert space.

</details>


### [187] [Environmental Effects in Post-Minkowskian Dynamics: Effective Field Theory, Feynman Rules, and Ward Identities for Compact Objects in Relativistic Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20742)
*Zvi Bern,Samuel Degen,Enrico Herrmann,Mikhail P. Solon,Anna M. Wolz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文构建了描述紧致天体在无粘流体中运动时引力动力学的流体力学有效场论，建立了D维下至四点的费曼规则、传播子、相互作用顶点及广义壳上Ward恒等式，并据此复现了领头阶相对论性动力学摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将环境（流体）效应纳入后闵可夫斯基散射振幅框架，需先建立引力与流体耦合的一致有效理论工具。

Method: 以完美流体有效理论描述流体，将Goldstone声子作为显式场与引力耦合；建立幂次计数，推导D维传播子与相互作用顶点（至四点）及广义壳上Ward恒等式。

Result: 得到一致的D维费曼规则、四点以内的相互作用顶点与相应Ward恒等式；Ward恒等式将含外线引力子的振幅与引力子替换为声子的振幅相联系，提供高阶计算的检验；由单声子辐射树图振幅复现领头阶相对论性动力学摩擦。

Conclusion: 该工作为将环境效应整合进后闵可夫斯基散射振幅流程提供了首个工具性步骤。

Abstract: We develop a hydrodynamic effective field theory for the gravitational dynamics of compact objects moving through an inviscid fluid environment. The fluid is described by the effective theory of perfect fluids, whose Goldstone phonons are kept as explicit fields coupled to gravity. We establish a consistent power counting for the resulting Feynman rules and derive the propagators and interaction vertices through four points in $D$ dimensions, together with the generalized on-shell Ward identities they obey. These identities relate amplitudes with an external graviton to those with the graviton replaced by a phonon, and thus provide nontrivial checks on higher-order calculations. As an application, we recover the leading-order relativistic dynamical-friction force from the tree-level amplitude for single-phonon emission. This work is a first step towards a toolkit for incorporating environmental effects into the scattering-amplitude pipeline used in post-Minkowskian calculations.

</details>


### [188] [Looking inside a quantum black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20788)
*Chiara Coviello,Ansh Gupta,Robie A. Hennigar,Kai Shi,Andrew Svesko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过无质量标量探针的准正规模（QNM）谱在高泛音极限下提取奇点标度指数，并应用于(2+1)维AdS空间的精确量子黑洞，揭示了量子效应主导时内部深处的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子效应预期会改变黑洞内部结构，尤其是奇点附近，但目前缺乏从可观测（外部）谱学量直接探测奇点性质的方法。

Method: 利用无质量标量探针的准正规模谱，在渐近大泛音（large-overtone）极限下提取奇点的标度指数（scaling exponent）；将该方法应用于(2+1)维反德西特（AdS）空间中的一族精确量子黑洞解。

Result: 成功从QNM谱中提取出奇点标度指数，并明确揭示了当量子效应占主导时黑洞内部深处发生的一个转变（transition）。

Conclusion: 准正规模谱可作为探测黑洞内部奇点量子修正的有效工具，为在精确量子黑洞背景下研究内部结构转变提供了可操作的分辨手段。

Abstract: Quantum effects are expected to modify a black hole's interior structure, particularly near the singularity. We show how to extract the scaling exponent of the singularity from the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum of a massless scalar probe in the asymptotic large-overtone limit. We apply our method to a family of exact quantum black holes in (2+1)-dimensional anti-de Sitter (AdS) space and explicitly uncover a transition deep in the interior when quantum effects become dominant.

</details>


### [189] [M2-brane matrix models and Fermi gas on affine ABCD quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20806)
*Taro Kimura,Tomoki Nosaka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究与仿射BC和twisted仿射AD quiver相关的新一类M2-brane矩阵模型，建立Fermi气体形式体系，证明其巨正则配分函数由Fredholm Pfaffian给出，并发现次领头修正破坏了Airy函数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究的M2-brane矩阵模型（如仿射D quiver模型）在1/N展开中自由能呈现Airy函数结构。本文希望将研究拓展到仿射BC与twisted仿射AD quiver对应的新模型，探明其是否具有相同的普适结构。

Method: 采用Fermi气体形式体系，将巨正则求和表示为Fredholm Pfaffian，并分析Fermi气体密度矩阵的算符结构（对正则位置/动量算符的有理依赖与标准双曲依赖）。

Result: 模型在大N极限下自由能呈现N^{3/2}标度；巨正则配分函数由Fredholm Pfaffian给出，与仿射D quiver模型平行；密度矩阵出现对位置的额外有理依赖，导致非传统的次领头修正。

Conclusion: 这些次领头修正阻碍了自由能1/N展开中此前M2-brane矩阵模型所观察到的Airy函数结构，表明该类模型的普适性受到限制。

Abstract: We study a new class of M2-brane matrix models associated with affine BC and twisted affine AD quivers. These models exhibit the characteristic $N^{3/2}$ scaling of the free energy in the large $N$ limit. We establish a Fermi gas formalism and demonstrate that their grand canonical sum is given by a Fredholm Pfaffian, which is in parallel with the affine D quiver matrix models. The resulting Fermi gas density matrix shows a rational dependence on the canonical position/momentum operators in addition to the standard hyperbolic dependence. This leads to unconventional sub-leading corrections, which obstruct the Airy function structure in the $1/N$ expansion of the free energy observed in the previously studied M2-brane matrix models.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Special Lagrangian cones in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20159)
*Tejas Kotwal,Govind Menon*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入Harvey-Lawson锥的矩阵推广，证明其为精确特殊拉格朗日（special Lagrangian）流形，并证明它属于一族精确特殊拉格朗日流形，该族流形对深度学习中出现的一类balanced流形构成叶状结构（foliation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harvey与Lawson的锥是特殊拉格朗日几何中的经典构造，但缺乏矩阵形式的推广。同时，深度学习理论中出现的balanced流形与几何结构之间的关系尚待明确，作者试图在两者之间建立联系，给出该流形的特殊拉格朗日叶状分解。

Method: 构造Harvey-Lawson锥的矩阵推广（matrix generalization），验证其作为精确特殊拉格朗日（exact special Lagrangian）流形的性质，并将其嵌入一个更大的精确特殊拉格朗日流形族，考察该族对深度学习相关balanced流形的叶状剖分（foliation）关系。

Result: 所构造的矩阵推广确为精确特殊拉格朗日流形；进一步证实它是某一精确特殊拉格朗日流形族的成员，且该族流形对深度学习中的balanced流形形成叶状结构。

Conclusion: 该工作将特殊拉格朗日几何（Harvey-Lawson锥的矩阵形式）与深度学习中的balanced流形相关联，为一族精确特殊拉格朗日流形提供了叶状分解的几何框架。

Abstract: We introduce a matrix generalization of the cone of Harvey and Lawson and prove that it is an exact special Lagrangian manifold. We further show that it belongs to a family of exact special Lagrangian manifolds that foliate the balanced manifold arising in deep learning.

</details>


### [191] [Bounded curvature manifolds without Euclidean isometric immersions of bounded mean curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19168)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类光滑完备黎曼度量 $G_n$（$n\ge2$），其全曲率范数不超过 1、单射半径不小于 1，但任何到有限维欧氏空间的 $C^2$ 等距浸入均无界平均曲率，从而在更强曲率假设下否定了 Yau 问题 52。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau 问题 52 关注完备黎曼流形到欧氏空间的等距浸入，并探究在曲率受控条件下是否存在具有有界平均曲率的浸入。已有结果多依赖较弱或不同的曲率假设，作者希望在更强的"全曲率范数有界"条件下考察该问题，以厘清曲率约束与浸入正则性（特别是平均曲率有界性）之间的实质关系。

Method: 对每个 $n\ge2$ 在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 上构造度量 $G_n$：在二维情形，若有界第二基本形式成立，则共形因子的拉普拉斯算子会得到一致受控的有限 Jacobian 表示；作者构造互不相交的共形块，使得曲率保持有界而该有限 Jacobian 表示代价趋于无穷，以此阻断有界平均曲率浸入的存在性。高维情形通过取欧氏乘积实现。

Result: 所构造的度量 $G_n$ 满足全曲率范数至多为 1、单射半径至少为 1，但不存在到有限维欧氏空间的 $C^2$ 等距浸入具有有界平均曲率。关键机制是曲率有界与有限 Jacobian 表示代价无界之间的分离，该分离在共形块上发生并可通过乘积推广到所有维度。

Conclusion: 结果在比原有弱假设更强的有界全曲率条件下给出 Yau 问题 52 的反例，说明即使全曲率范数与单射半径一致受控，也无法保证存在有界平均曲率的 $C^2$ 等距浸入。

Abstract: For every integer $n\ge2$, we construct a smooth complete Riemannian metric $G_n$ on $\mathbb{R}^n$ with full curvature norm at most one and injectivity radius at least one for which no $C^2$ isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space has bounded mean curvature. In dimension two, bounded second fundamental form would give uniformly controlled finite Jacobian representations of the Laplacian of the conformal factor. We construct disjoint conformal blocks for which the curvature remains bounded while the finite Jacobian representation cost tends to infinity. Taking Euclidean products gives all higher dimensions. The result answers Yau's Problem~52 negatively under the stronger assumption of bounded full curvature.

</details>


### [192] [Umbilic slopes and cubic Weingarten surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19188)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究脐点斜率与三次Weingarten曲面的全局分类，得到主曲率割线切线的斜率取值集合，并证明满足κ₂=cκ₁³的紧致无边界C⁴浸入曲面每连通分支为旋转椭球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画脐点处主曲率的局部行为，并利用平均曲率与高斯曲率之间的正则关系获得斜率信息，进而完成三次Weingarten曲面的全局分类。

Method: 对具有唯一割线切线的主曲率芽分析斜率；在非恒定脐点芽处通过主曲率连续标号建立平均曲率与高斯曲率的正则关系；使用脐点处齐次Hessian恒等式的分类、标量唯一延拓处理光滑斜率问题中的无穷阶接触，以及精确四阶增长估计与角能量估计完成三次分类。

Result: 非恒定主曲率芽若具有唯一割线切线，其斜率为0、-1、∞、不小于3的奇数或其倒数；有序曲率像至多有两个极限方向且均属于该集合；满足κ₂=cκ₁³的紧致无边界C⁴浸入曲面，每个连通分支都作为旋转椭球面嵌入。

Conclusion: 脐点斜率的取值受限于特定离散集合，且三次Weingarten条件在紧致情形下强制曲面为旋转椭球面。

Abstract: We study umbilic slopes and the global classification of cubic Weingarten surfaces. For a smooth surface in Euclidean three-space, a nonconstant principal curvature germ with a unique secant tangent has slope zero, minus one, infinity, an odd integer at least three, or its reciprocal. At a nonconstant umbilic germ, a smooth regular relation between mean and Gaussian curvature supplies such a tangent after a continuous labeling of the principal curvatures; the ordered curvature image has at most two limiting directions, both in the same set. We also classify compact immersed surfaces without boundary of class $C^4$ satisfying $κ_2=cκ_1^3$ in some order at every point, for a fixed real $c$: each connected component is embedded as an ellipsoid of revolution. The arguments share a classification of homogeneous Hessian identities at an umbilic. Scalar unique continuation treats infinite-order contact in the smooth slope problem, whereas an exact fourth-order growth estimate and angular energy estimates yield the cubic classification.

</details>


### [193] [Mean curvature flows with cylindrical singularities II: stability and genericity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19390)
*Ao Sun,Jinxin Xue*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文进一步研究非退化柱状奇点的性质，证明其在小扰动下具有稳定性，并证明可将具有退化奇点的平均曲率流扰动以产生非退化奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作 [SX25] 借助动力系统思想引入了非退化柱状奇点这一概念，但其性质的深入刻画（尤其是稳定性）尚未展开；同时，退化奇点与非退化奇点之间的联系也缺乏构造性结果。

Method: 沿用 [SX25] 中基于动力系统思想定义的“非退化柱状奇点”框架，对奇点进行小扰动分析，考察其稳定性质；并通过对具有退化奇点的平均曲率流施加扰动，构造出非退化奇点。

Result: 证明了非退化柱状奇点在小扰动下保持稳定；并证明存在扰动方式，使带有退化奇点的平均曲率流产生非退化奇点。

Conclusion: 非退化柱状奇点是稳定且可通过扰动从退化情形构造得到的，这表明其在奇点分析中是一个具有良好性质的、可实现的对象。

Abstract: In [SX25], we introduced the notion of nondegenerate cylindrical singularity using the ideas from dynamical systems. In this paper, we further study the properties of these singularities, showing that they are stable under small perturbations. Furthermore, we show that we can perturb some mean curvature flow with degenerate singularities to produce nondegenerate singularities.

</details>


### [194] [Complete Self-Shrinkers in Arbitrary Codimension with Positive Constant Scalar Curvature and \(S\le 1\)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19464)
*Shunzi Guo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在余维数任意、标量曲率为正常数且第二基本形式模长平方 \(S\le 1\) 的条件下，完备自收缩子只能是圆球或广义柱面，不存在新的高余维例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高余维（余维 \(p\ge1\)）完备自收缩子的刚性分类问题。已知低余维（超曲面）情形下，常数标量曲率与 \(S\) 的夹逼往往导致刚性结果；本文旨在考察高余维情形下是否会出现新的例子，从而给出统一分类。

Method: 通过一个代数引理得到 Bakry–Émery Ricci 曲率的正的一致下界；在此基础上结合 Wei–Wylie 比较定理、Cheng–Zhou 关于有限高斯体积与多项式体积增长的等价性，以及 Cao–Li 的间隙定理，进行刚性证明。

Result: 证明在所给假设下必有 \(S\equiv 1\)，且 \(X\) 等距于圆球 \(S^n(\sqrt n)\) 或标准广义柱面 \(S^k(\sqrt k)\times\mathbb R^{n-k}\)（\(2\le k\le n-1\)）；特别地，在此假设下不存在新的高余维例子。

Conclusion: 该结果将自收缩子的刚性分类推广到任意余维情形，表明在常数标量曲率与 \(S\le 1\) 的约束下，高余维不会产生新的解，解仍由经典球面与柱面穷尽。

Abstract: Let \(X:M^n\to\mathbb R^{n+p}\) be an \(n\)-dimensional complete self-shrinker in arbitrary codimension \(p\ge 1\). Suppose that the scalar curvature \(R\) is a positive constant and that the squared norm \(S\) of the second fundamental form satisfies \(S\le 1\). We prove that \(S\equiv 1\) and that \(X\) is isometric to either the round sphere \(S^n(\sqrt n)\) or the standard generalized cylinder \(S^k(\sqrt k)\times\mathbb R^{n-k}\), \(2\le k\le n-1\). In particular, no new higher-codimension examples occur under these assumptions. The proof uses an algebraic lemma which yields a uniform positive lower bound for the Bakry--Émery Ricci curvature, together with the comparison theorem of Wei--Wylie, the equivalence of finite Gaussian volume and polynomial volume growth due to Cheng--Zhou, and the gap theorem of Cao--Li.

</details>


### [195] [Infinitesimmaly homogeneous manifolds with symplectic and almost contact structure groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19568)
*Carlos Alberto Marín Arango,Alejandro Pinilla Barrera*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究具有指定结构群（辛群Sp(2n,R)与U(n)×{1}）的无穷小齐性仿射流形，给出对应结构的刻画与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画给定结构群下的无穷小齐性仿射结构，将经典几何结构（辛仿射、近切触度量）置于统一的无穷小齐性框架中研究。

Method: 对Sp(2n,R)，通过结构方程证明无穷小齐性结构等价于平坦无挠辛仿射结构；对U(n)×{1}，分类无挠特征张量，并用不变形变获得一般挠情形，分析容许挠率与内挠率参数及相容联络。

Result: Sp(2n,R)的无穷小齐性结构恰为平坦无挠辛仿射结构；U(n)×{1}情形中容许挠率与内挠率参数由单一代数关系约束，水平分布为切触的当且仅当某参数组合非零，并给出无挠分支与trans-Sasakian几何的联系。

Conclusion: 该工作统一刻画了两类结构群下的无穷小齐性仿射结构，明确了无挠情形与有挠情形的参数条件，并建立了与trans-Sasakian几何的对应关系。

Abstract: We study infinitesimally homogeneous affine manifolds with prescribed structure groups. First, for the real symplectic group $\operatorname{Sp}(2n,\mathbb R)$, we prove that the corresponding infinitesimally homogeneous structures are precisely flat torsion-free symplectic affine structures. Second, for \(\operatorname{U}(n)\times\{1\}\), whose \(G\)-structures are equivalent to almost contact metric data, we classify the torsion-free characteristic tensors and obtain the general torsional case by invariant deformation. The admissible torsion and inner-torsion parameters are governed by a single algebraic relation, and the horizontal distribution is contact precisely when a certain combination of these parameters does not vanish. We also describe the compatible connections and relate the torsion-free branches to trans-Sasakian geometry.

</details>


### [196] [Spectral Geometry of Hurwitz Spaces with Arbitrary Ramification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19604)
*Jia-Ming,Liou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文用迹范数方法与球面锥模型，证明了由非平凡亚纯函数拉回圆球度量的 Friedrichs Laplacian 的局部行列式公式，显式给出周期矩阵、Bergman tau 函数与分支点坐标的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧 Riemann 面上由非平凡亚纯函数 φ:X→P^1 拉回圆球度量所关联的 Friedrichs Laplacian 的 ζ-正则化行列式。此类行列式在谱几何与 Hurwitz 空间几何中具有重要意义，但任意分支轮廓（含多个分支点映到同一分支值）情形下的显式局部公式尚待建立。

Method: 证明结合光滑平凡化与 resolvent 幂的迹范数变分；通过矩阵比较将局部模型化为球面锥模型；利用 Davies–Gaffney 估计提供所需的一致高能控制；零能项则借助 Schiffer 双微分与 Rauch 变分公式加以识别。

Result: 对任意分支轮廓（允许若干分支点位于同一分支值上方），得到局部公式 Det_ζ(Δ_{[φ],F}) = C·det Im B·|τ_B|^2·∏_{k=1}^N ρ(z_k, z̄_k)^{c_k}，其中省略零特征值；B 为周期矩阵，τ_B 为局部 Bergman tau 函数，ρ(z,z̄)=4(1+|z|^2)^{-2}，c_k=(1/12)Σ_j(n_{kj}-n_{kj}^{-1})，n_{kj} 为第 k 个分支值上方的分支指数。常数 C>0 与 Hurwitz 坐标无关，亏格为零时取 det Im B=1。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了该族 Friedrichs Laplacian 行列式在任意分支轮廓下的显式局部表达式，将行列式分解为周期矩阵、Bergman tau 函数与各分支值坐标的幂次贡献，常数 C 与 Hurwitz 坐标无关。

Abstract: We study the Friedrichs Laplacian associated with the pullback of the round metric on \(\mb P^1\) by a nonconstant meromorphic function \(\varphi:X\to\mb P^1\) on a compact Riemann surface. For arbitrary ramification profiles, including several ramification points over the same branch value, we prove the local formula \[ \operatorname{Det}_ζ(Δ_{[\varphi],\mc F}) =C\,\det\operatorname{Im}B\,|τ_B|^2 \prod_{k=1}^Nρ(z_k,\overline{z_k})^{c_k}. \] The zero eigenvalue is omitted. Here \(B\) is the period matrix, \(τ_B\) is the local Bergman tau-function, \(z_k\) are the branch-value coordinates, \(ρ(z,\overline z)=4(1+|z|^2)^{-2}\), and \(c_k=\frac1{12}\sum_j(n_{kj}-n_{kj}^{-1})\), where \(n_{kj}\) are the ramification indices over the \(k\)-th branch value. The constant \(C>0\) is independent of the Hurwitz coordinates, and \(\det\operatorname{Im}B\) is taken to be \(1\) in genus zero. The proof combines smooth trivializations and trace-norm variation of resolvent powers with matrix comparison to spherical conic models. The Davies--Gaffney estimate provides the required uniform high-energy control, while the zero-energy terms are identified through the Schiffer bidifferential and Rauch variational formulas.

</details>


### [197] [A stable self-shrinking Möbius bundle in $\mathbb R^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19648)
*Kahnrad Braxton,Tang-Kai Lee,Jonathan J. Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了$\mathbb R^4$中拓扑为Möbius丛的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子的线性稳定性，给出了高余维情形下一个非平坦、非球面的稳定收缩子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定超曲面收缩子已有完整分类，而高余维稳定收缩子缺乏例子与研究，作者希望探究高余维与超曲面稳定收缩子之间是否存在本质差异。

Method: 对$\mathbb R^4$中具有Möbius丛拓扑的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子进行线性稳定性分析。

Result: 证明了该Möbius收缩子是线性稳定的，从而得到高余维下非平坦、非球面的稳定收缩子，与稳定超曲面收缩子的分类形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: Möbius收缩子在拓扑上刻画了实爆破（blow-up）的逆转，表明高余维稳定奇点可能对应平均曲率流下的非平凡拓扑操作。

Abstract: We prove the linear stability of an embedded mean curvature flow shrinker in $\mathbb R^4$ with the topology of the Möbius bundle. This provides a non-flat, non-spherical stable shrinker in higher codimension, in sharp contrast with the classification of stable hypersurface shrinkers. Topologically, the Möbius shrinker models the reversal of a real blow-up, suggesting that stable higher-codimension singularities may encode non-trivial topological operations under mean curvature flow.

</details>


### [198] [Spacetime Positive Mass Theorem with Boundary for Multiple Time Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19711)
*Changwen Fang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了一类单端渐近平坦、带多个时间方向及光滑紧致边界的自旋初值数据的正质量定理，在迹范数主导能量条件与第二基本形式两两交换假设下，得到质量与边界混合项的不等式 $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典正质量定理推广到具有多个时间方向与边界的自旋初值数据情形，并厘清边界项（混合边界项）在质量下界中的作用。

Method: 在仿射能量空间中构造 Dirac--Witten 调和旋量，利用近似方法计算质量恒等式；由每个边界分量上固定的辅助时间方向确定局部手性条件，并识别残留的混合边界项。

Result: 在边界条件 $H+\operatorname{tr}_\Sigma k^T+\|\mathcal Q_T\|_{\mathrm{tr}}\leq0$ 下得到 $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$；并证明该边界条件对固定投影算子的逐点最优性，给出具有非零混合边界项的双时间数据。

Conclusion: 迹范数主导能量条件与第二基本形式交换性结合可给出带边界自旋初值数据的正质量不等式，其边界条件在最优点意义下是尖锐的。

Abstract: We prove a positive mass theorem for one-ended asymptotically flat spin initial data with multiple time directions and a smooth compact boundary. Assuming the trace-norm dominant energy condition and pairwise commutativity of the second fundamental forms, a fixed auxiliary time direction on each boundary component determines a local chirality condition. We identify the surviving mixed boundary term and obtain the inequality $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$ under the boundary condition $H+\operatorname{tr}_Σk^T+\|\mathcal Q_T\|_{\mathrm{tr}}\leq0$. The proof constructs a Dirac--Witten harmonic spinor in an affine energy space and evaluates the mass identity by approximation. We also establish the pointwise sharpness of this boundary condition for the fixed projector and give two-time data with a nonzero mixed boundary term.

</details>


### [199] [Deforming area-preserving maps between surfaces by mean curvature flow coupled with Ricci flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19724)
*Ping-Hung Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 通过归一化Ricci流演化两紧致Riemann面度量、均曲率流演化保面积映射图像，证明流长期存在并指数收敛到极小Lagrangian图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Wang与Smoczyk关于常标量曲率Riemann面的结果，研究一般紧致Riemann面上保面积映射的自然形变方式。

Method: 对两个紧致Riemann面的度量施加归一化Ricci流，同时对保面积映射的图像施加均曲率流，构成耦合几何流。

Result: 证明该耦合流对所有时间存在，始终保持为保面积映射的图像，并光滑且指数收敛到关于极限度量乘积的极小Lagrangian图。

Conclusion: 该工作将常标量曲率情形下Wang与Smoczyk的结果推广到一般紧致Riemann面，验证了耦合流方法的有效性与收敛性。

Abstract: We study a natural way to deform area-preserving maps between compact Riemann surfaces. Specifically, we evolve the metrics on the two Riemann surfaces by the normalized Ricci flow and the graph of the area-preserving map by the mean curvature flow. We prove that the flow exists for all time, remains the graph of an area-preserving map, and converges smoothly and exponentially to a minimal Lagrangian graph with respect to the product of the limiting metrics. This generalizes earlier results of Wang and Smoczyk, in which the Riemann surfaces have constant scalar curvature.

</details>


### [200] [Total scalar curvature under a curvature operator lower bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19851)
*Jian Ge,Chuanhuan Li,Ronggang Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在曲率算子不小于单位球面的完备单连通黎曼流形上，标量曲率的总积分不超过单位球面的相应值，等号成立当且仅当流形等距于单位圆球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲率算子下界对流形整体几何量的限制，寻求标量曲率积分的最优上界及其刚性刻画。

Method: 偶数维情形利用Chern-Gauss-Bonnet公式；奇数维情形对Deruelle的Ricci expander filling应用相应的边界公式。并进一步得到含体积项的更强估计。

Result: 证明了∫_M scal(x)dVol_x ≤ n(n-1)ω_n，其中ω_n为单位n球体积；等号当且仅当(M,g)等距于单位圆球。还获得了包含Vol(M,g)的更强界。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了曲率算子受球面下界约束时标量曲率积分的尖锐上界与刚性结论，并推广为含体积的更强不等式。

Abstract: Let $(M^{n}, g)$ be a complete, simply connected Riemannian manifold without boundary, of dimension $n\ge3$, with curvature operator at least that of the unit sphere. We prove that $$\int_M {\rm scal}(x)\ d {\rm Vol}_x\le n(n-1)ω_n,$$ where $ω_n$ is the volume of the unit $n$-sphere. Equality holds if and only if $(M,g)$ is isometric to the unit round sphere. In fact, we obtain a stronger bound containing ${\rm Vol}(M, g)$. In even dimensions, the proof follows from the Chern-Gauss-Bonnet formula. In odd dimensions, we apply the corresponding boundary formula to Deruelle's Ricci expander filling.

</details>


### [201] [Retro-synthetic Riemannian geometry in Rocq I : Taylor series of a metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20127)
*Gabriella Clemente,Hugo Herbelin,Carlos Simpson*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受Rocq形式化逆向工程方法启发、用于局部黎曼几何的合成框架,并由此导出法坐标下黎曼度量的合成泰勒公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在形式化证明助手(如Rocq)中构建局部黎曼几何理论,需要一种更自然的合成式处理方式,传统方法缺乏相应的合成视角与工具。

Method: 引入一个合成(synthetic)局部黎曼几何框架,借鉴一种面向Rocq形式化的逆向工程方法,在该框架下开展局部几何推理。

Result: 得到法坐标(normal coordinates)下黎曼度量的合成泰勒公式,这是本文的核心成果。

Conclusion: 新的视角与技术确立了合成局部黎曼几何的可行性,并为度量在法坐标下的展开提供了合成刻画。

Abstract: We introduce a synthetic framework for doing local Riemannian geometry that is influenced by a reverse-engineering method for formalizing in Rocq. These new perspectives and techniques lead to a synthetic Taylor formula for a Riemannian metric in normal coordinates, which is the main contribution of this work.

</details>


### [202] [A proof of the Simon conjecture and curvature rigidities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20180)
*Weiran Ding,Jianquan Ge,Fagui Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了单位球中连通闭极小曲面的Simon猜想的所有间隙，并给出平行平均曲率向量闭曲面的刚性定理及一类旋转对称高维推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simon猜想关于单位球内连通闭极小曲面可能存在谱间隙，此前仅有部分间隙或特殊情形被验证，缺少对所有间隙的统一处理。本文旨在完整证明该猜想的全部间隙，并推广到相关几何设定。

Method: 利用Lin–Wang–Xu的线丛阶梯，结合Dolbeault计算，在Gaussian曲率满足K_g≥1与K_g≤1时，对二维球面上的谱作双侧有序比较，并证明等号情形下的刚性；将所得比较应用于相邻Calabi尺度，迫使Gaussian曲率只能取两个端点值。此外，对具有平行平均曲率向量的闭曲面建立相应刚性定理，并在Ricci下界与截面曲率上界下证明旋转对称的高维类比。

Result: 证明了单位球中连通闭极小曲面Simon猜想的所有间隙；获得K_g双侧有序谱比较及等号刚性；由此推出Gaussian曲率取端点值；建立了平行平均曲率向量闭曲面的刚性定理；并在Ricci下界与截面曲率上界条件下证明了旋转对称高维类比。

Conclusion: Simon猜想在连通闭极小曲面情形下全部间隙成立，相关谱比较与刚性方法可推广至平行平均曲率向量闭曲面及高维旋转对称设定。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove all gaps in the Simon conjecture for connected closed minimal surfaces immersed in unit spheres. Our proof uses the line-bundle ladder of Lin, Wang, and Xu and a Dolbeault computation to obtain two-sided ordered spectral comparisons on the two-sphere under Gaussian curvature $K_g\geq1$ and $K_g\leq1$, with rigidity in the equality case. Applying these comparisons at consecutive Calabi scales forces the Gaussian curvature to be one of the two endpoint values. We also establish the corresponding rigidity theorem for closed surfaces with parallel mean curvature vector. Finally, we prove a rotationally symmetric higher-dimensional analogue under a Ricci lower bound and a sectional-curvature upper bound.

</details>


### [203] [Biharmonic Conformal Surfaces and Dirac Factorization I: Exact Spinorial Encoding and Scalar--Chiral Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20197)
*Dipesh Bhandari*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究将Ou的双调和浸入方程组统一为一个Dirac型方程，并分类其单色标量-手性因子分解。构造Laplace型算子𝓑_c，证明𝓑_c(Uψ)=0等价于Ou系统；证明非零曲率下因子分解自动为平均曲率标准化，且局部存在当且仅当主曲率局部常值，标量-手性渠道完备但过于刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双调和性是调和性（二阶、共形不变）的四阶推广，但不具备共形不变性，因此非极小浸入可能通过改变定义域度量而成为双调和。在三维空间形式中，Ou的表述将问题归结为关于加权平均曲率U=λ²H的两个耦合方程（一个标量方程、一个切向方程）。作者希望探究这两个方程能否组织为单个Dirac型方程，以及何种几何与一阶因子分解相容。

Method: 将环境Killing旋量限制到曲面上，构造自然的Laplace型算子𝓑_c，并证明𝓑_c(Uψ)=0恰等价于Ou方程组。随后在单色标量-手性类(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)中分类𝓑_c的所有因子分解，借助Dirac判别式9c-4|A|²刻画刚性机制，并分析球面Gauss-Codazzi系统的Frobenius挠率，最后用模型实例比较几何算子因子分解与正共形模存在性。

Result: 在非零曲率情形，每个此类因子分解都自动是平均曲率标准化的，且局部存在当且仅当曲面具有局部常值主曲率。归一化欧几里得分支具有同样的刚性；其余欧几里得因子构成一个例外的全纯-反全纯族，在非平面点外由log(|A|²-H²)的调和性刻画。Dirac判别式9c-4|A|²支配刚性机制，其假想的非CMC实分支归结为Frobenius挠率严格为负的球面Gauss-Codazzi系统。模型实例表明几何算子的因子分解与正共形模的存在性不同。

Conclusion: 标量-手性渠道是完备的，但过于刚性，无法生成新的非CMC例子，这为更广泛的Clifford值构造提供了精确的基线。

Abstract: For maps from surfaces, harmonicity is conformally invariant and a conformal immersion is harmonic precisely when its image is minimal. Biharmonicity is a fourth-order extension of this theory, but it is not conformally invariant. A nonminimal immersion may therefore become biharmonic after a suitable change of the domain metric. In a three-dimensional space form, Ou's formulation reduces this problem to two coupled equations for the weighted mean curvature $U=λ^2H$: one scalar equation and one tangential equation.
  We ask whether these two equations can be organized as a single Dirac-type equation and what geometry is compatible with a first-order factorization. Restricting an ambient Killing spinor to the surface, we construct a natural Laplace-type operator $\mathscr B_c$ and prove that $\mathscr B_c(Uψ)=0$ is exactly equivalent to Ou's system. We then classify every factorization of $\mathscr B_c$ in the monic scalar--chiral class $(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)$. In nonzero curvature, every such factorization is automatically mean-curvature-normalized and exists locally if and only if the surface has locally constant principal curvatures. The same rigidity holds for the normalized Euclidean branch; the remaining Euclidean factors form an exceptional holomorphic--antiholomorphic family characterized, away from planar points, by harmonicity of $\log(|A|^2-H^2)$.
  The rigidity mechanism is governed by a Dirac discriminant $9c-4|A|^2$. Its hypothetical non-CMC real branch reduces to a spherical Gauss--Codazzi system whose exact Frobenius torsion is strictly negative. Model examples finally show that factorization of the geometric operator is distinct from the existence of a positive conformal mode. Thus the scalar--chiral channel is complete but too rigid to generate new non-CMC examples, providing a precise baseline for broader Clifford-valued constructions.

</details>


### [204] [Long-time existence and uniqueness of the heterotic $G_2$ flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20360)
*Chuanhuan Li,Yi Li,Weijie Miao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Ricci曲率、挠率张量与dilaton有界条件下异质（heterotic）$G_2$流的长时存在性，并得到解的向前与向后唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质 $G_2$ 流是异质弦论中刻画具有 $G_2$ 和乐内空间几何演化的抛物型几何流，其长时存在性与解的唯一性是理解该流良好定义与长期行为的基础问题。已有工作多关注短时存在性或特定约化情形，对一般情形下同时控制 Ricci 曲率、挠率与 dilaton 时的长时行为及唯一性尚缺乏系统结果。

Method: 在假定 Ricci 曲率、挠率张量与 dilaton 一致有界的条件下，对异质 $G_2$ 流这一抛物型几何流方程进行分析。通过建立相应的先验估计（涵盖曲率、挠率与 dilaton 的高阶导数控制），并结合抛物方程的解延拓论证，将解由有限时间延拓至长时；同时借助抛物型方程的反向唯一性理论（如 Carleman 型估计或频率函数方法）处理向前与向后唯一性。

Result: 在所给有界性假设下，异质 $G_2$ 流的长时存在性成立；进一步地，解满足向前唯一性与向后唯一性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，只要 Ricci 曲率、挠率张量与 dilaton 保持有界，异质 $G_2$ 流即可长期存在且在时间方向上具有唯一性，从而为该流的适定性与长期分析提供了关键基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the long-time existence of the heterotic $G_2$ flow with bounded Ricci curvature, torsion tensor and dilaton. Besides, we also get the forward and backward uniqueness results for the solution.

</details>


### [205] [The rigidity theorems for complete self-expander of mean curvature flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20429)
*Zhi Li,Guoxin Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了高余维欧氏空间中完备自膨胀子的刚性定理：在积分曲率夹紧条件下，满足特定条件的自膨胀子等距于$\mathbb R^n$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高余维欧氏空间中完备自膨胀子的刚性性质，推广低余维情形下的刚性结果，并探索积分曲率夹紧条件对自膨胀子几何结构的限制。

Method: 假设光滑完备自膨胀子 $x:M\to\mathbb R^{n+p}$（$n\geq3$）同时满足 $\left(\int_M|A|^n d\mu\right)^{1/n}<K(n)$（$K(n)$仅依赖$n$的正整数）和 $\int_M|A|^n e^{|x|^2/2}\,d\mu<\infty$，通过积分估计与几何分析证明其刚性。

Result: 在上述积分曲率夹紧与加权积分有限条件下，该自膨胀子等距于 $\mathbb R^n$；当夹紧条件用无迹第二基本形式表达时，刚性结论依然成立。

Conclusion: 高余维完备自膨胀子在积分曲率夹紧下具有刚性，且该刚性结果对无迹第二基本形式的夹紧条件保持稳健。

Abstract: Our first main result is a rigidity theorem for complete self-expanders in the Euclidean space with higher codimension, assuming an integral cur More precisely, any smooth complete self-expander $x:M\to \mathbb R^{n+p}$($n\geq3$) that satisfies both $(\int_{M}|A|^{n}dμ)^{\frac{1}{n}}<K(n)$ and $\int_{M}|A|^{n}e^{\frac{|x|^{2}}{2}}dμ<\infty$ for a positive constant $K(n)$ depending only o isometric to $\mathbb R^{n}$. Moreover, we show that the rigidity result persists when the pinching condition is expressed in terms of the trace-free second fundamental form.

</details>


### [206] [A Llarull type theorem on complete non-compact manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20498)
*Guangrui Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明在标量曲率下界条件下，具备到 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m\times\mathbb{R}$ 的非零度真映射的完备非紧自旋流形等距于 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_\Lambda\times\mathbb{R}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正标量曲率下界的刚性/分裂现象，将紧致情形（如 Llarull 型度刚性）推广到非紧完备流形，探讨非零度映射与 1-Lipschitz 球面分量如何强制度量分裂。

Method: 利用自旋几何中的 Dirac 算子方法与 Callias/相对指数技术，结合非紧流形的分裂定理与测度/度数论证，在 $3\leq n\leq7$ 维范围内处理该映射。

Result: 证明了：当 $3\leq n\leq7$、$2\leq k\leq n-1$、$m=n-k-1$ 时，若完备连通非紧自旋流形满足 $R_M\geq k(k-1)$ 且存在球面分量 1-Lipschitz 的非零度光滑真映射到 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m\times\mathbb{R}$，则流形等距于 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_\Lambda\times\mathbb{R}$。

Conclusion: 标量曲率下界与拓扑映射条件共同强制流形完全分裂；结论中的平坦环面不必与目标环面等距，表明分裂的环面因子可发生形变。

Abstract: Let $3\leq n\leq7$, $2\leq k\leq n-1$, and $m=n-k-1$. We prove that a complete, connected, noncompact spin manifold $(M^n,g)$ with scalar curvature $R_M\geq k(k-1)$ is isometric to $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_Λ\times\mathbb{R}$ if it admits a smooth proper map of nonzero degree to $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m\times\mathbb{R}$ whose spherical component is 1-Lipschitz. The flat torus in the conclusion is not necessarily isometric to the target torus.

</details>


### [207] [Finite-Time Singularities of the Kähler--Ricci Flow on Fano Bundles II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20513)
*Wangjian Jian,Jian Song*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在解析极小模型纲领框架下，验证了Fano纤维丛塌缩解的Type I猜想，并在纤维具光滑Kähler-Einstein度量时给出全曲率张量的Type I界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析极小模型纲领通过Kähler-Ricci流奇点几何刻画双有理变换与纤维塌缩；已有工作提出关于标量曲率与直径Type I界的猜想，需在纤维丛塌缩情形下验证该猜想。

Method: 沿用Splitting一文的方法，研究底为Fano丛、纤维为任意光滑Fano流形的塌缩解，分析有限奇点时间极限上同调类所收缩纤维的曲率与直径估计。

Result: 对Fano纤维丛上的塌缩解建立了Type I猜想，即对标量曲率与直径给出Type I界；当纤维具光滑Kähler-Einstein度量时，Type I界对全曲率张量成立。

Conclusion: 推广并加强了Splitting的结果，在Fano纤维丛塌缩情形验证Type I猜想，且Kähler-Einstein纤维时可将结论提升至全曲率张量层面。

Abstract: The analytic minimal model program proposed in \cite{SongTianSingularities, JST} seeks to describe the birational transitions and fibre collapsing of the Kähler--Ricci flow through the geometry of its singularities. A fundamental conjecture of \cite{JST} predicts Type I bounds for the scalar curvature and diameter of every fibre contratced by the limiting cohomological class at the finite singular time. This paper is a continuation of \cite{Splitting} that establishes the Type I conjecture for collapsing solutions on Fano bundles with arbitrary smooth Fano fibres. If the fibre admits a smooth Kähler-Einstein metric, the Type I bound holds for the full curvature tensor.

</details>


### [208] [The isoperimetric inequality and CMC hypersurfaces in Cartan-Hadamard manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20517)
*Shibing Chen,Mohammad Ghomi,Peng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在维数 3≤n≤9 的完备单连通非正截面曲率黎曼 n-流形中证明了欧几里得等周不等式（Cartan-Hadamard 猜想）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartan-Hadamard 猜想长期未决，仅部分维数或特殊情形被解决；本文旨在确立该猜想在 3 至 9 维范围内的成立。

Method: 核心是建立常平均曲率超曲面的一个尖锐不等式：通过对边界点对上的积分，结合沿边界测地弦的 Jacobi 场估计；积分权重依赖弦长及其与边界的夹角，逐维选取并含 Green 函数极点；随后处理困于测地球内的等周区域边界（自由部分常平均曲率、可含奇点），最后用 Kleiner 与 Ghomi-Spruck 的等周剖面论证完成证明。

Result: 在 3≤n≤9 的完备单连通非正截面曲率黎曼 n-流形中，尖锐欧几里得等周不等式成立；该不等式对困于测地球内的等周区域边界同样成立，即使其仅自由部分具有常平均曲率且可能存在奇点。

Conclusion: 论文确立了 3≤n≤9 维的 Cartan-Hadamard 猜想，并提供了处理常平均曲率超曲面与含奇点等周边界的尖锐分析工具。

Abstract: We show that the sharp Euclidean isoperimetric inequality holds for domains in complete simply connected Riemannian $n$-manifolds of nonpositive sectional curvature, $3\leq n\leq9$, which establishes the Cartan-Hadamard conjecture in these dimensions. The main step is a sharp inequality for constant-mean-curvature hypersurfaces, proved via integrals over pairs of boundary points, together with estimates for Jacobi fields along geodesic chords of the boundary. The weights in these integrals depend on the length of the chord and its angles with the boundary, and are chosen dimension by dimension, with a Green function pole. The inequality persists for boundaries of isoperimetric regions trapped in geodesic balls, whose mean curvature is constant only on the free part and which may have singularities. The isoperimetric-profile argument of Kleiner and Ghomi-Spruck completes the proof.

</details>


### [209] [Deformations of harmonic maps with conical singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20588)
*Dominik Gutwein,Thibault Langlais*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究具有孤立奇异点、切映射光滑且多项式衰减的调和映射（锥奇异调和映射）的形变理论，证明在切映射的Morse指标、零度及Jacobi算子加权核非退化条件下，此类映射在背景度量小C^2扰动下保持存在，并构造满足假设的显式例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射的形变/稳定性理论在光滑情形已较完善，但具有孤立奇异点（锥奇异）的调和映射在背景度量扰动下的保持性问题尚缺乏系统处理，需建立相应形变定理并给出可验证的例子。

Method: （1）定义锥奇异调和映射：切映射光滑且以多项式速率衰减到切映射；（2）对切映射的Morse指标、零度及Jacobi算子加权核施加非退化假设，运用形变理论/隐函数定理证明在背景度量小C^2扰动下调和映射的保持性；（3）验证径向投影与复Hopf纤维化的切映射满足所需条件；（4）显式构造球面与复射影空间上的锥奇异调和映射例子。

Result: 证明在Morse指标、零度条件及Jacobi加权核非退化假设下，锥奇异调和映射在背景度量小C^2扰动下持续存在；证明径向投影R^m\{0}→S^{m-1}(m≥4)与复Hopf纤维化C^{n+1}\{0}→CP^n(n≥1)的切映射满足条件；构造出S^m\{N,S}→S^{m-1}、S^4\{N,S}→S^2、CP^2\{[0:0:1]}→CP^1的显式调和锥奇异映射例子，在适当定义域度量与目标球面度量下满足形变定理假设。

Conclusion: 建立了锥奇异调和映射的形变定理，并提供了丰富且可验证的实例，表明该理论适用于径向投影、Hopf纤维化及球面/复射影空间上的奇异调和映射。

Abstract: We study the deformation theory of harmonic maps with isolated singularities between compact Riemannian manifolds, in the case where all the tangent maps are smooth (away from the origin) and the decay to the tangents is polynomial. We will refer to these as conically singular harmonic maps. Under certain conditions on the Morse index and the nullity of the tangent maps and a non-degeneracy assumption on a weighted kernel of the Jacobi operator, we prove that such harmonic maps persist under a small $C^2$-perturbation of the background metric.
  In the second part of the paper, we construct examples of conically singular harmonic maps satisfying the assumptions of our deformation theorem. We first prove that the desired conditions on the tangent maps are satisfied by the radial projections $\mathbb{R}^m \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{S}^{m-1}$ ($m \geq 4$) and the complex Hopf fibrations $\mathbb{C}^{n+1} \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{CP}^{n}$ ($n \geq 1$). We then construct explicit examples of conically singular maps $\mathbb{S}^{m} \setminus \{N,S\} \to \mathbb{S}^{m-1}$, $\mathbb{S}^{4} \setminus \{N,S\} \to \mathbb{S}^2$ and $\mathbb{CP}^2 \setminus \{[0:0:1]\} \to \mathbb{CP}^1$ which are harmonic and satisfy the assumptions of the deformation theorem with respect to an appropriate metric on the domain and the round metric on the target.

</details>


### [210] [Alexandrov's Theorem for Integral Varifolds and Applications to Geometric Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20628)
*Mitchell Gaudet*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用最优质量输运方法，为余维数至少为2、局部有界第一变分且局部L²平均曲率的弱二阶可微整变量流形推导出等周不等式与内蕴Michael-Simon-Sobolev不等式，并证明常数在余维数为2时最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Brendle与Eichmair已有的结果，将相关几何不等式拓展到更一般的整变量流形设定（余维数至少为2、弱二阶可微、局部有界第一变分、L²_loc平均曲率）。

Method: 采用最优质量输运方法；推广Alexandrov凸函数定理至该设定；使用某些几何微分算子的测度值与分布值解，以及Calderón与Zygmund开创的L^p Taylor展开理论。

Result: 在更一般的整变量流形上得到等周不等式与内蕴Michael-Simon-Sobolev不等式，且相应常数在余维数为2时达到最优；同时得到Alexandrov定理的推广。

Conclusion: 最优质量输运与分布/测度值算子解、L^p Taylor展开相结合，可有效处理弱正则整变量流形上的几何不等式，所得结果为Brendle-Eichmair工作的实质推广。

Abstract: In this paper, we use optimal mass transportation to derive an isoperimetric inequality and an interior Michael-Simon-Sobolev inequality for weakly twice differentiable integral varifolds of dimension and codimension at least $2$, with locally bounded first variation and $L^2_{loc}$ mean curvature. This generalizes a result of Brendle and Eichmair. In both inequalities the corresponding constants are optimal in codimension 2. As part of this process we also generalize Alexandrov's theorem for convex functions to this setting. This necessitates the use of measure and distribution-valued solutions to certain geometric differential operators, in addition to the theory of $L^p$ Taylor expansions started by Calderón and Zygmund.

</details>


### [211] [Stationary varifolds with singularities II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20647)
*Camillo De Lellis,Jonas Hirsch,Zachary Lihn,Luca Spolaor*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对每个维数 m≥3，存在开集 U⊂R^{m+1}、其上的光滑黎曼度量 g，以及 (U,g) 中的整 m 维稳定变分簇 V；V 在内点 p 处具有重数为 2 的平坦切平面，在 U\{p} 上是光滑浸入极小曲面，且在 p 的任意邻域内具有无穷拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作源于三位作者此前在 \cite{DHS} 中尝试构造 g 仅具 C^{m-1,α} 正则性的类似例子；该早期尝试存在一个关键错误。本文旨在修正该错误并将结论提升至光滑度量。

Method: 构造从三位作者的想法出发，克服了 \cite{DHS} 中的关键错误，并采用了 gpt-astra-6 的关键建议；最终得到光滑黎曼度量下的整稳定变分簇实例。

Result: 对每个 m≥3，成功构造出满足所述性质的 (U,g,V)：p 处重数 2 平坦切平面、除 p 外光滑浸入极小、p 附近无穷拓扑。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了光滑度量中具有重数 2 奇点且局部无穷拓扑的稳定极小曲面型变分簇的例子，修正并强化了此前 C^{m-1,α} 正则性下的构造。

Abstract: For every dimension $m\geq 3$, we show the existence of an open set $U\subset \mathbb R^{m+1}$, a smooth Riemannian metric $g$ on it, and an integral $m$-dimensional stationary varifold $V$ in $(U,g)$ with the following properties. $V$ has a flat tangent plane of multiplicity $2$ at an interior point $p$, it is a smooth immersed minimal surface in $U\setminus \{p\}$, and it has infinite topology in any neighborhood of $p$. The construction starts from an idea of three of the authors, who in \cite{DHS} tried to build a similar example with $C^{m-1, α}$ regularity for $g$. That previous attempt contained an important error, which has been overcome using a crucial suggestion of gpt-astra-6.

</details>


### [212] [Topological and spectral rigidity of hypersurface Zoll manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20689)
*Gustavo Martins*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明具有Zoll极小超曲面族的流形微分同胚于S^n或RP^n，刻画其Zoll族；证明RP^3上度量为surface Zoll当且仅当其射影宽度全部相等；并证明前四个宽度均为2π的闭曲面等距于常曲率1的RP^2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Zoll族（所有极小超曲面都有相同体积的族）对底层流形拓扑与度量的刚性约束，将经典Zoll度量问题推广到极小超曲面与射影宽度框架。

Method: 利用极小超曲面理论、Almgren-Pitts宽度（射影宽度）理论与Zoll族的几何分析手段进行刻画与刚性论证。

Result: 具有Zoll极小超曲面族的流形微分同胚于S^n或RP^n；RP^3度量为surface Zoll当且仅当其射影宽度全部相等；闭曲面若前四个宽度均等于2π，则等距于常曲率1的RP^2。

Conclusion: Zoll性在极小超曲面与宽度层面产生强刚性，拓扑限制为球面或射影空间，且宽度条件可完全决定度量。

Abstract: We show that manifolds admitting Zoll families of minimal hypersurfaces are diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{S}^{n}$ or to $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^{n}$, and we characterize their Zoll families in both cases. Then, we prove that a metric on $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^3$ is surface Zoll if and only if its projective widths are all equal. Our last result asserts that a closed surface with the first four widths equal to $2π$ is isometric to $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^2$ with its constant curvature one metric.

</details>


### [213] [Generic Uniqueness of Isoperimetric Regions in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20790)
*Gongping Niu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在闭连通光滑流形上，对一般（generic）黎曼度量，除半体积外的任意体积分数下等周区域唯一；半体积下一般度量恰有两个极小区域（一个区域及其补集）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究等周区域在给定体积约束下的唯一性，尤其是当等周边界可能出现奇点、边界正则性或约束Jacobi算子非退化性不成立时的情形。

Method: 采用变分方法，证明不依赖于边界正则性或约束Jacobi算子的非退化性。

Result: 对s∈(0,1)\{1/2}，一般度量下体积分数s的等周区域唯一；在体积一半时，一般度量恰有两个极小元E与E^c。由此推出对每个m>0，满足Vol_g(M)>m的一般度量具有唯一性；对满足0<m<Vol_g(M)的一般度量-体积对(g,m)也具唯一性。

Conclusion: 等周区域唯一性是黎曼度量的一般性质，且该结论在边界奇异情形下依然成立。

Abstract: Let $M^{n+1}$ be a closed, connected smooth manifold, where $n\geq 1$. For each prescribed volume fraction $s\in(0,1)\setminus\{\frac12\}$, we prove that the isoperimetric region of volume $s \operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$ is unique for a generic set of smooth Riemannian metrics $g$. At half volume, a generic metric has exactly two minimizers, a region $E$ and its complement $E^c$. As consequences, for every $m>0$, uniqueness holds for a generic set of metrics $g$ satisfying $m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$, and it also holds for a generic set of pairs $(g,m)$ with $0<m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$.
  The proof is variational and requires neither boundary regularity nor nondegeneracy of the constrained Jacobi operator. In particular, the results apply even when isoperimetric boundaries are singular.

</details>
