### [214] [Tachikawa's second conjecture implies the Auslander-Reiten conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19172)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明立川第二猜想蕴含Artin代数上的Auslander-Reiten猜想，从而使得多个同调猜想彼此等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同调代数中存在若干著名猜想（Auslander-Reiten、广义Nakayama、Auslander-Gorenstein、Nakayama、Gorenstein-projective、立川第一与第二猜想），它们之间的关系尚不完整，尤其缺乏立川第二猜想与Auslander-Reiten猜想之间的直接蕴含关系。

Method: 对交换Artin环上的Artin代数，利用代数的双重平凡扩张（two-fold trivial extension）构造论证，证明立川第二猜想蕴含Auslander-Reiten猜想。

Result: 证明了立川第二猜想 ⟹ Auslander-Reiten猜想；结合已知蕴含关系，得出Auslander-Reiten猜想、广义Nakayama猜想、Auslander-Gorenstein猜想、Nakayama猜想、Gorenstein-projective猜想与立川第二猜想全部等价，且立川第二猜想蕴含立川第一猜想。

Conclusion: 这些同调猜想构成一个等价类，立川第二猜想处于核心地位，其成立将统一解决上述诸猜想。

Abstract: We prove that Tachikawa's second conjecture implies the Auslander-Reiten conjecture for artin algebras over a commutative artinian ring. The proof uses the two-fold trivial extension of an algebra. Together with known implications, it follows that the Auslander-Reiten conjecture, the generalized Nakayama conjecture, the Auslander-Gorenstein conjecture, the Nakayama conjecture, the Gorenstein-projective conjecture, and Tachikawa's second conjecture are all equivalent, and that Tachikawa's second conjecture implies his first.

</details>


### [215] [The Arthur-Packet Support Equality for Real Reductive Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19197)
*Jiawei Yang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了 microlocal packet 等于两步 Jordan induction packet 的支撑，完成了 AIMV 的猜想等号部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIMV 已证 microlocal packet 包含于两步 Jordan induction packet 的支撑中并猜想等号成立；本文旨在证明反向包含以确立等号。

Method: 先平移至足够 positive 的 translate，使 ABV 空间的相关连通分支具有公共 flag-variety model；用 perverse devissage 将问题归约为奇异支撑蕴含，该蕴含由 AIMV microstalk comparison 背后的 incidence calculation 得到；再对 packet 中各成员用 coherent continuation 回到原参数。

Result: 证明了反向包含，从而 microlocal packet 恰等于两步 Jordan induction packet 的支撑。

Conclusion: AIMV 猜想中的等号成立，microlocal packet 与两步 Jordan induction packet 的支撑一致。

Abstract: Let $ψ$ be a real Arthur parameter and let $ψ_2$ be the unipotent parameter in a fixed Jordan decomposition. Adams, Ionov, Mason-Brown, and Vogan proved that the microlocal packet of $ψ$ is contained in the support of the two-step Jordan induction of the packet of $ψ_2$, and conjectured equality. We prove the reverse inclusion. The argument first passes to a sufficiently positive translate, where the relevant connected components of the ABV spaces have a common flag-variety model. Perverse devissage reduces the problem to a singular-support implication, which follows from the incidence calculation underlying the AIMV microstalk comparison. Coherent continuation for individual packet members then returns the result to the original parameter.

</details>


### [216] [On the Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda conjecture on formal degrees for $\mathrm{G_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19430)
*Yugo Takanashi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 通过三重性扭化内窥特征恒等式，证明了 $\mathrm{G}_2$ 上形式次数的 Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立 $\mathrm{G}_2$ 上形式次数的 Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda 猜想，并发展与三重性 $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ 表示论相关的基础结果。

Method: 利用三重性 $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ 与其内窥群 $\mathrm{G}_2$ 之间的扭化内窥特征恒等式，并应用 $E_6$ 型伴随群。

Result: 由扭化内窥特征恒等式推出了 $\mathrm{G}_2$ 上形式次数的 Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda 猜想，并建立了三重性 $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ 表示论的一些基础结果。

Conclusion: 扭化内窥特征恒等式与 $E_6$ 型伴随群的应用为研究 $\mathrm{G}_2$ 的表示论及形式次数猜想提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We prove that the twisted endoscopic character identities between triality $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ and the endoscopic group $\mathrm{G}_2$ imply the Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda conjecture on formal degrees for $\mathrm{G}_2$. In the course of proving the main result, we also establish some fundamental results on representation theory of $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ with triality. A key feature of our approach is an application of the adjoint group of type $E_6$.

</details>


### [217] [A Comparison Theorem for Parahoric Character Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19860)
*Zhihang Yu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为parahoric子群上的特征层特征函数建立了Jordan分解公式，在q足够大时这些函数等于相应深水平Deligne--Lusztig特征乘以(-1)^{dim G}，将Lu90推广到正深度，并证明了广义深水平Green函数与特征层函数的正交性，给出了G^{F^2}特征限制到G^{F}的重数的层论表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Deligne--Lusztig理论及Lusztig关于特征层特征函数的Jordan分解结果从深度零情形推广到正深度（深水平）情形，从而处理parahoric子群上特征层的相应理论。

Method: 在parahoric子群上定义的特征层框架下，建立其特征函数的Jordan分解公式；对充分大的q比较这些函数与深水平Deligne--Lusztig特征；并借助Green函数与特征层函数的正交性进行论证。

Result: 得到特征函数关于parahoric子群的Jordan分解公式；证明当q充分大时特征函数等于(-1)^{dim G}乘以对应的深水平Deligne--Lusztig特征，从而推广Lu90至正深度；证明了广义深水平Green函数与特征层函数的正交性；并给出G^{F^2}特征限制到G^{F}的多重性的层论表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作把特征层特征函数的Jordan分解与深水平Deligne--Lusztig特征理论统一起来，为正深度情形提供了正交性工具，并给出了限制特征多重性的层论解释。

Abstract: We establish a Jordan decomposition formula for the characteristic functions of the character sheaves on parahoric subgroups defined in \cite{INY25}. For a sufficiently large $q$, these functions are $(-1)^{\dim G}$ times the corresponding deep level Deligne--Lusztig characters, extending \cite{Lu90} to positive depth. We also prove the orthogonality for the generalized deep level Green functions and the character sheaves functions. As an application, we present a sheaf-theoretic expression to the multiplicity of the $G^{F^2}$ character restricted to $G^{F}$.

</details>


### [218] [A refined multiplication formula in 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20049)
*Ming Ding,Fan Xu,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文在Hom-有限2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius外三角范畴上证明了Wang-Wei-Zhang簇特征（cluster character）的乘法公式，并给出冻结项（frozen term）的表示及主系数网格关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有Wang-Wei-Zhang在一维情形以及Keller-Plamondon-Qin在（稳定）2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius或三角范畴中的乘法公式，本文旨在将其推广到更一般的设置，并应用于Auslander-Reiten网格与主系数箭图。

Method: 设$\mathcal C$为带簇倾斜对象的Hom-有限2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius外三角范畴，假定其稳定范畴关于诱导的簇倾斜对象具有可构造锥（constructible cones），在此条件下证明Wang-Wei-Zhang簇特征的乘法公式。

Result: 证明了该乘法公式，推广了Wang-Wei-Zhang（一维情形）与Keller-Plamondon-Qin（稳定2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius或三角范畴）的结果；用极小余核中间项（minimal cosyzygy middle term）的特征表达了Auslander-Reiten网格中的冻结项；对带主系数的无环箭图，在Higgs范畴中计算了冻结重数并得到显式主系数网格关系。

Conclusion: 该乘法公式统一并拓展了已有结果，并借助Higgs范畴给出无环箭图主系数网格关系的显式计算。

Abstract: We prove a multiplication formula for the Wang-Wei-Zhang cluster character on a Hom-finite $2$-Calabi--Yau Frobenius extriangulated category $\mathcal C$ with a cluster-tilting object under the assumption that its stable category has constructible cones with respect to the induced cluster-tilting object. This formula generalizes those obtained by Wang-Wei-Zhang for $\mathcal C$ in the one-dimensional case and by Keller-Plamondon-Qin for (stably) $2$-Calabi-Yau Frobenius or triangulated categories. As a consequence, we express the frozen term in an Auslander-Reiten mesh with the character of a minimal cosyzygy middle term. For acyclic quivers with principal coefficients, we compute these frozen multiplicities in Higgs categories and obtain explicit principal coefficient mesh relations.

</details>


### [219] [Unitary Branching for $\mathfrak{sl}(m\vert n), \mathfrak{osp}(m\vert 2n)$ and $F(4)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20332)
*Steffen Schmidt*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文确定基本经典李超代数 sl(m|n)、osp(m|2n) 与 F(4) 在限制到偶子代数时不可约酉表示的分支规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本经典李超代数的不可约酉表示在限制到偶子代数时的分支（分解成分与重数）是该领域的核心问题，但现有 Dirac 方法受限于正系统需与相应实形式相适应，难以统一处理，尤其在高非典型度情形。

Method: 结合 Huang-Pandžić-Dirac 算子 D_{g,g_0} 的 Dirac 不等式与由 Dirac 形式导出的 Freudenthal 递推的超类比；并通过奇反射传输 Dirac 不等式、确定相应修正项，将 Dirac 方法推广到不与相关实形式相适应的正系统。

Result: 得到确定出现的偶成分及其分支重数的有限递推程序，并给出重数的封闭公式，且对非典型度一致成立；对 sl(m|n)，修正后的 Dirac 不等式还对所有与酉表示相关的正系统给出了酉性刻画。

Conclusion: 分支问题可通过修正 Dirac 不等式与超 Freudenthal 递推统一求解，且该方法在非典型度上一致有效，并为 sl(m|n) 的酉性提供新刻画。

Abstract: We determine the branching of irreducible unitary representations of the basic classical Lie superalgebras $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, $\mathfrak{osp}(m|2n)$, and $F(4)$ under restriction to their even subalgebras. The proof combines Dirac inequalities for the Huang-Pandžić-Dirac operator $\operatorname{D}_{\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}_{\bar{0}}}$ with a super analogue of Freudenthal's recursion derived from the Dirac formalism. We extend the Dirac method to positive systems that are not adapted to the relevant real form by transporting the Dirac inequality through odd reflections and determining the resulting correction terms. This yields finite recursive procedures that determine the occurring even constituents and their branching multiplicities, together with closed formulas for the latter, uniformly in the degree of atypicality. For $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, the corrected Dirac inequalities also give a characterization of unitarity for all positive systems relevant to unitary representations.

</details>


### [220] [Representations of formal Lie groups and Lie pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20547)
*Fulin Chen,Binyong Sun,Chuyun Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文发展形式Lie群与Lie对的表示论基础，构造形式流形上函数空间的Lie对模，并证明形式Lie群的表示范畴同构于对应Lie对的模范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式Lie群是微分几何中Lie群在形式框架下的推广，类比于代数几何中的形式群概型。作者在前作中已建立形式Lie群基本理论，包括将形式Lie群与Lie对等同的形式Lie定理，但表示论尚待发展。

Method: 在形式流形上的函数空间上构造Lie对的模结构，并建立形式Lie群表示与Lie对模之间的对应关系。

Result: 证明了形式Lie群的表示范畴与对应Lie对的模范畴同构。

Conclusion: 形式Lie群的表示论可通过Lie对的模范畴刻画，将前作的形式Lie定理从结构层面推广到表示层面。

Abstract: Formal Lie groups, which generalize Lie groups in differential geometry, are analogous to formal group schemes in algebraic geometry. In a previous paper, we established the basic theory of formal Lie groups, including a formal Lie theory theorem that identifies formal Lie groups with Lie pairs. In this paper, we develop the foundations of the representation theory of formal Lie groups and Lie pairs. In particular, we construct modules of Lie pairs on function spaces over formal manifolds and prove that the category of representations of a formal Lie group is isomorphic to the category of modules of the corresponding Lie pair.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Skein theory and deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19329)
*Noah Snyder,Benjamin Spencer*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 利用skein理论的"识别"定理对pivotal（及ribbon）monoidal范畴的形变进行分类：证明quantum G_2作为pivotal范畴的无穷小形变仅来自q的变化，并给出Deligne S_t的Kuperberg型新刻画，证明其（除t=0）ribbon无穷小形变仅来自t的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对给定量子群/张量范畴的形变进行刚性刻画，即判定其形变是否被参数q或t的变动完全穷尽，这类刚性问题是范畴化量子不变量理论中的核心问题。作者希望将Kuperberg关于quantum G_2的skein刻画推广，并为Deligne S_t建立类似刻画。

Method: 基于skein理论的"识别"（recognition）定理：先推广Kuperberg对quantum G_2的skein刻画，据此分析其作为pivotal范畴的无穷小形变；同时建立一个新的Kuperberg型skein刻画用于Deligne范畴S_t，再分析其作为ribbon范畴的无穷小形变。

Result: 证明了quantum G_2作为pivotal范畴的任意无穷小形变均通过改变q得到，该结论在一般（generic）q以及q为单位根时的tilting模范畴情形下均成立（需排除少数小单位根）；并证明S_t（t≠0）作为ribbon范畴的任意无穷小形变均来自t的变化。

Conclusion: skein理论识别方法可有效证明此类范畴的形变刚性：quantum G_2与Deligne S_t的无穷小形变分别被参数q、t完全分类，表明这些结构在相应pivotal/ribbon意义下具有强刚性。

Abstract: We use skein theoretic `recognition' theorems to classify deformations of certain pivotal or ribbon monoidal categories. We first show that a slight generalization of Kuperberg's characterization of quantum $G_2$ shows that any infinitesimal deformation of quantum $G_2$ as a pivotal category arises by varying $q$. This result applies both at generic $q$, and for the category of tilting modules at $q$ a root of unity (provided we exclude a few small roots of unity). Second, we prove a new Kuperberg-like characterization of Deligne's $S_t$ and use it to show that any infinitesimal deformation of $S_t$ (excluding $t=0$) as a ribbon category comes from varying $t$.

</details>


### [222] [Braided Hopf algebroids and Lie algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19563)
*Xiao Han*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 在辫monoidal范畴中构造辫Hopf algebroid，研究其图形表示与Ehresmann-Schauenburg结构，引入辫Lie-Rinehart代数及万有包络代数，并构造辫jet Hopf algebroid，给出对偶配对与斜配对结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Hopf algebroid、Lie-Rinehart代数与jet理论推广到辫monoidal范畴框架，以刻画辫Hopf Galois扩张（量子主丛）上的不变向量场与微分结构。

Method: 利用图形演算（graphical representation）在域k上的辫monoidal范畴中构造辫Hopf algebroid；定义辫Lie-Rinehart代数并在对称条件下研究其万有包络代数；结合三角Hopf代数模范畴与辫Ehresmann-Schauenburg Hopf algebroid研究右不变向量场；在源、靶子代数属于辫中心时构造辫jet Hopf algebroid。

Result: 辫Ehresmann-Schauenburg Hopf algebroid的右不变向量场作为Lie-Rinehart代数同构于量子主丛（Hopf Galois扩张）上的H-等变向量场；辫右不变向量场的k阶万有包络代数与辫Hopf algebroid的第k阶jet空间之间存在对偶配对，在有限性条件下其万有包络代数与jet Hopf algebroid之间存在斜配对。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了辫monoidal范畴中Hopf algebroid、Lie-Rinehart代数与jet结构的统一框架，推广了已有Lie-Rinehart代数定义，并为量子主丛上的不变向量场与jet理论提供了对偶刻画。

Abstract: We construct braided Hopf algebroids in a braided monoidal category over a field $k$ and study its properties using graphical representation. Then we study Ehresmann-Schauenburg Hopf algebroids assocaited to braided Hopf Galois extensions.
  We introduce braided Lie-Rinehart algebras which generalize the definition in \cite{ALP24, ALP23, ALP25} and study its universal enveloping algebra under symmetric condition. In particular, we introduce Lie-Rinehart algebras (braided Lie-algebroids) associated with braided Hopf algebroids in the category of the module of a triangular Hopf algebra. We then focus on the case for braided Ehresmann-Schauenburg Hopf algebroids and study their right invariant vector fields, which turn out to be isomorphic to the $H$-equivariant vector fields on the quantum principal bundle (Hopf Galois extension) as Lie-Rinehart algebras.
  Finally, we introduce braided jet Hopf algebroids associate to braided Hopf algebroids in the case that the source and target subalgebra belong to the braided center. Moreover, we show there is a dual pairing between the $k$-order universal enveloping algebra of the braided right invariant vector fields and the $k$-th jet space of a braided Hopf algebroid and further more a skew pairing between the universal enveloping algebra of its braided right invariant vector fields and its jet Hopf algebroid under a finiteness condition.

</details>


### [223] [Affine quantum Schur--Weyl duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19608)
*Qiang Fu,Jun Hu*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明了扩展仿射Hecke代数在仿射张量空间上的自然右作用总是忠实的；在特征0且ε非单位根时，自然同态ξ_r是同构，验证了DDF的猜想3.8.8，并借此证明了DDF关于仿射量子Schur代数中心的猜想；同时证明了仿射量子Schur代数在Noether交换环上左右Noether，验证了DY注1.7中的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在研究扩展仿射Hecke代数H_△(r)_K与仿射量子Schur代数S_△(n,r)_K之间的关系，特别是验证文献DDF中提出的关于自然同态ξ_r为同构的猜想3.8.8、关于仿射量子Schur代数中心的猜想，以及DY注1.7中关于Noether性质的猜想。

Method: 设K为含可逆元素ε的交换环，H_△(r)_K为Hecke参数ε的A型扩展仿射Hecke代数，Ω_K^{⊗r}为仿射张量空间，S_△(n,r)_K为相应仿射量子Schur代数。首先证明H_△(r)_K在Ω_K^{⊗r}上的自然右作用是忠实的；进而假设K为特征0的域且ε非单位根，证明自然代数同态ξ_r: H_△(r)_K → End_{S_△(n,r)_K}(Ω_K^{⊗r})^{op}为同构；最后在K为Noether交换环时证明S_△(n,r)_K左右Noether。

Result: 证明了H_△(r)_K在Ω_K^{⊗r}上的自然右作用总是忠实的；在K为特征0的域且ε非单位根、n≥2时，ξ_r为同构，从而验证了DDF猜想3.8.8；作为应用证明了DDF[5.2.4]中关于仿射量子Schur代数中心的猜想；并证明了当K为Noether交换环时S_△(n,r)_K是左右Noether的，验证了DY注1.7中的猜想。

Conclusion: 扩展仿射Hecke代数在仿射张量空间上的自然右作用具有忠实性；在特征0且ε非单位根条件下，H_△(r)_K与S_△(n,r)_K在Ω_K^{⊗r}上的自同态代数对偶同构，由此解决DDF关于中心的猜想；并确立仿射量子Schur代数在Noether交换环上的左右Noether性质，验证了DY的猜想。

Abstract: Let $\mathpzc K$ be an arbitrary commutative ring containing an invertible element $\varepsilon$. Let ${\mathcal H}_{\!\vartriangle\!}(r)_{\mathpzc K}$ be the extended affine Hecke algebra of type $A$ with Hecke parameter $\varepsilon$, let $Ω_{\mathpzc K}^{\otimes r}$ be the affine tensor space, and let ${\mathcal S}_{\!\vartriangle\!}(n,r)_{\mathpzc K}$ be the corresponding affine quantum Schur algebra. We first prove that the natural right action of ${\mathcal H}_{\!\vartriangle\!}(r)_{\mathpzc K}$ on $Ω_{\mathpzc K}^{\otimes r}$ is always faithful.
  Assume further that $\mathpzc K$ is a field of characteristic $0$ and that $\varepsilon$ is not a root of unity. We prove that, for any $n\geq 2$, the natural algebra homomorphism $ξ_r:{\mathcal H}_{\!\vartriangle\!}(r)_{\mathpzc K}\rightarrow\operatorname{End}_{{\mathcal S}_{\!\vartriangle\!}(n,r)_{\mathpzc K}}(Ω_{\mathpzc K}^{\otimes r})^{\mathrm{op}}$ is an isomorphism. This proves Conjecture~3.8.8 of \cite{DDF}. As an application, we prove the conjecture formulated in \cite[5.2.4]{DDF} concerning the center of the affine quantum Schur algebra.
  We also prove that ${\mathcal S}_{\!\vartriangle\!}(n,r)_{\mathpzc K}$ is left and right Noetherian whenever $\mathpzc K$ is a Noetherian commutative ring, which verify a conjecture in \cite[Rem. 1.7]{DY}.

</details>


### [224] [Unitary TQFTs, unitary disk-like $n$-categories, and higher Hilbert spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19713)
*Greyson Wesley*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文提出"酉盘状n-范畴"概念,将其完整有限版本定义为有限(n+1)-Hilbert空间,并在n=1,2时验证该提议,给出沿向(1+1)D与(2+1)D酉TQFT的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为任意维数给出有限Hilbert空间的范畴化定义,并以此统一刻画全扩张酉TQFT的局部场数据,推广Schommer-Pries关于(1+1)D TQFT的分类。

Method: 在盘状n-范畴上赋予反射结构与球状迹(诱导正定配对),定义酉盘状n-范畴;通过构造等距等价的函子性对应关系,验证n=1,2情形;并研究完全非完整3-Hilbert空间、pivotal dagger 2-范畴的严格化以及盘状n-范畴间的函子与高阶变换。

Result: 证明完整有限酉盘状1-范畴与2-范畴分别等距等价于有限2-与3-Hilbert空间且该等价函子性成立;n=1时恢复Schommer-Pries分类的酉细化(H*-Morita等价类),n=2时范畴化为用H*-multifusion范畴的H*-Morita等价类分类沿向(2+1)D酉TQFT。

Conclusion: 完整有限酉盘状n-范畴可作为有限(n+1)-Hilbert空间的合理定义,并在低维情形被验证,为高维酉TQFT分类提供统一框架。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of a unitary disk-like $n$-category, which is a disk-like $n$-category equipped with a reflection structure and a sphere trace inducing positive-definite pairings. Since a finite unitary disk-like $n$-category is defined to be the local field data of a fully extended $(n+1)$D unitary TQFT, we propose a complete finite unitary disk-like $n$-category as the definition of a finite $(n+1)$-Hilbert space for all $n$. For $n=1$ and $n=2$ we verify this proposal, proving that complete finite unitary disk-like 1- and 2-categories are isometrically equivalent to finite 2- and 3-Hilbert spaces respectively, and that these equivalences are functorial. For $n=1$ we recover a unitary refinement of Schommer-Pries' classification of oriented $(1+1)$D TQFTs in terms of $\mathrm{H}^*$-Morita equivalence classes of $\mathrm{H}^*$-algebras, and for $n=2$ we categorify this to classify oriented $(2+1)$D unitary TQFTs by $\mathrm{H}^*$-Morita equivalence classes of $\mathrm{H}^*$-multifusion categories. Along the way, we investigate fully incomplete 3-Hilbert spaces, prove a strictification result for pivotal dagger 2-categories, and define functors and higher transformations between disk-like $n$-categories.

</details>


### [225] [Magma Automorphisms and Quasi-Linear Cycle Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20028)
*Nigel P. Byott,Edgar Jasko*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文将Rump关于有限拟线性循环集是否可收缩的问题，重新表述为特定带基点原群的自同构问题，并提出一个可推出肯定答案的猜想，同时给出理论与计算证据支持该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rump于2016年提出：是否每个有限拟线性循环集都是可收缩的？该问题长期悬而未决，本文旨在为这一问题提供新的等价表述与可能的解决路径。

Method: 将原问题转译为某些带基点原群（pointed magmas）的自同构性质，进而提出关于这些原群自同构的猜想；通过理论论证与计算实验收集支持该猜想的证据。

Result: 给出了Rump问题的原群自同构等价表述，并提出一个蕴含Rump问题肯定回答的猜想；同时提供了理论证明与计算机搜索两方面的支持性证据。

Conclusion: 若所提出的关于带基点原群自同构的猜想成立，则Rump问题将得到肯定回答；目前的理论与计算证据均支持该猜想，为该问题的最终解决提供了新方向。

Abstract: Rump asked in 2016 whether every finite quasi-linear cycle set is retractible. We reinterpret this question in terms of automorphisms of certain pointed magmas, and propose a conjecture on the automorphisms of these magmas which would imply an affirmative answer to Rump's question. We provide some theoretical and computational evidence in support of our conjecture.

</details>


### [226] [Hopf Images of Hopf algebra Coactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20118)
*Arnab Bhattacharjee*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文引入Hopf像（Hopf image）概念，即右余作用所通过的最小Hopf子代数，并研究其性质与计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopf代数余作用往往可通过更小的Hopf子代数分解，需要一个刻画"最小作用源"的工具来研究余作用的忠实性与结构。

Method: 定义Hopf代数的右余作用在代数上的Hopf像为余作用所通过的最小Hopf子代数；证明基本性质及诱导余作用的内忠实性；对由Yetter–Drinfeld模产生的Nichols代数余作用，证明Hopf像等于底层Yetter–Drinfeld模余作用所探测到的最小Hopf子代数；并利用bosonization构造具体例子。

Result: 建立了Hopf像的基本性质；证明Hopf像上的诱导余作用是内忠实的；给出Nichols代数情形下Hopf像的刻画；构造出由bosonization产生的非平凡真Hopf像的具体实例。

Conclusion: Hopf像为研究Hopf代数余作用的分解与忠实性提供了有效工具，并在Nichols代数与bosonization框架下有明确刻画与实例。

Abstract: We introduce the Hopf image of a right coaction of a Hopf algebra on an algebra as the smallest Hopf subalgebra through which the coaction factors. We establish its basic properties and show that the induced coaction on the Hopf image is inner-faithful. For coactions on Nichols algebras arising from Yetter--Drinfeld modules, we prove that the Hopf image is the smallest Hopf subalgebra detected by the underlying coaction on the Yetter--Drinfeld module. We give explicit examples of proper non-trivial Hopf images arising from bosonizations.

</details>


### [227] [On the Mext groups of sVec_R and sVec_H](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20153)
*Sean Sanford*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文计算实对称融合范畴 sVec_R 与 sVec_H 的极小非退化扩张群，发现二者均同构于 Klein 四元群 (Z/2Z)^2；同时分类了 FP 维数为 4 的实非退化编织融合范畴，确定了其中哪些是 Drinfeld 中心，并讨论了一个组织这些结构的同伦论猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究实对称融合范畴的非退化扩张群，是理解实范畴编织结构及其与拓扑/同伦论联系的基础问题，此前对 sVec_R 与 sVec_H 的极小非退化扩张群缺乏明确计算。

Method: 通过直接计算实对称融合范畴 sVec_R、sVec_H 的极小非退化扩张群；并借助 Frobenius-Perron 维数对实非退化编织融合范畴进行完整分类，逐一判定其是否为 Drinfeld 中心。

Result: sVec_R 与 sVec_H 的极小非退化扩张群均同构于 Klein 四元群 (Z/2Z)^2；完整分类了 FP 维数为 4 的实非退化编织融合范畴；精确确定了其中哪些范畴可实现为 Drinfeld 中心。

Conclusion: 文中最后提出一个同伦论猜想，用以统一组织上述分类与扩张群结构，为实融合范畴的非退化扩张提供整体性框架。

Abstract: We compute the groups of minimal nondegenerate extensions of the real symmetric fusion categories $\mathrm{sVec}_{\mathbb R}$ and $\mathrm{sVec}_{\mathbb H}$. We find that they are both isomorphic to the Klein-four group $(\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z})^2$. Along the way, we classify all nondegenerately braided fusion categories over $\mathbb R$ that have Frobenius-Perron dimension 4, and we determine exactly which of these categories is a Drinfeld center. We end the paper by discussing a homotopy-theoretic conjecture that organizes all these structures.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Hypersurfaces containing involutive cones in projective symplectic spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19427)
*Gabriel A. Guedes*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究射影空间中含有余维二锥的超曲面轨迹：给出锥与对极点刻画、构造关联空间、以Segre类积分表达关联度，并讨论关联映射的双有理性及二次情形的例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带接触结构与辛极性的射影空间中，刻画包含以超平面为支集的余维二锥的超曲面轨迹，并理解其参数化的关联映射几何性质。

Method: 利用"超平面H中次数≥2的超曲面X为对合子当且仅当它是顶点包含极点σ(H)的锥"的刻画；构造相应关联空间，计算维数，把关联度表示为Segre类积分。

Result: 在P³中除(m,d)=(2,2)外关联映射对一切d≥m≥2为双有理，P⁵中亦然；二次情形在各维数均为例外，P^{2n-1}中关联映射的一般度为2n。给出高维双有理性充分判据、P³的闭公式与P⁵的系数公式；P⁵中二次锥的度为移位二项式因子之积与一个36次不可约多项式之积。

Conclusion: 该工作系统描述了余维二锥超曲面轨迹的关联几何，明确了双有理性与例外情形，并揭示了P⁵二次锥度分解中移位因子的来源。

Abstract: Let $V$ be a complex symplectic vector space and let $\mathbb{P}(V)$ carry the induced contact structure and symplectic polarity. We study the loci of hypersurfaces containing codimension-two cones supported on hyperplanes. The starting point is the following characterization: a projective subvariety $X\subset\mathbb{P}(V)$ which is a hypersurface of degree at least two in a hyperplane $H$ is involutive if and only if it is a cone whose vertex contains the polar point $σ(H)$. We give a short proof, construct the corresponding incidence spaces, compute their dimensions and express the incidence degrees as Segre-class integrals. In $\mathbb{P}^3$ the incidence map is birational for every $d\ge m\ge 2$ except $(m,d)=(2,2)$, and the same holds in $\mathbb{P}^5$; the quadratic case is exceptional in every dimension, the incidence having generic degree $2n$ in $\mathbb{P}^{2n-1}$. We also give a sufficient criterion for birationality in higher dimension, closed formulas in $\mathbb{P}^3$ and coefficient formulas in $\mathbb{P}^5$. For quadric cones in $\mathbb{P}^5$ the degree is the product of shifted binomial factors and an irreducible polynomial of degree 36, and we explain where the shifted factors come from.

</details>


### [229] [Microlocal perverse schobers and Radon transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19692)
*Yuji Okitani*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文开启了反常层（perverse sheaves）的迷向（microlocal）研究，通过范畴化构造并提出迷向反常 schober 的通用定义，并证明其在特定超曲面上的 Radon 不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常 schober 是 Kapranov 与 Schechtman 提出的对反常层的范畴化。本文旨在对反常 schober 开展迷向（microlocal）研究，探索其与 Radon 变换的关系。

Method: 首先对 Perv(C,R)/Loc(C)（复直线上具有奇点集 R 的反常层模去局部系统）进行范畴化；据此提出支撑于超曲面芽的开余法向上的迷向反常 schober 的一般定义；关键观察是：虽然 2Perv(C,R)/2Loc(C) 的商是合理的范畴化，但所得理论在 Radon 变换下不具不变性，而所提出的范畴化可通过以普适方式修正这一失败情形而恢复。

Result: 证明了当超曲面为 C²_{x,y} 中的曲线 y^m = x^n 时 Radon 不变性成立，并阐明了该理论与反常 schober 的 Fourier 变换、周期 SOD 及球面 monad 之间的联系。

Conclusion: 本文启动了反常 schober 的迷向理论，提出了迷向反常 schober 的范畴化定义并验证了其在曲线 y^m = x^n 上的 Radon 不变性，同时建立了与 Fourier 变换、周期 SOD 和球面 monad 的关联。

Abstract: Perverse schobers are a categorification of perverse sheaves, originally proposed by Kapranov and Schechtman. The purpose of this paper is to initiate a microlocal study of perverse schobers. We first categorify $\operatorname{Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\operatorname{Loc}(\mathbb{C})$, the category of perverse sheaves on a complex line with singular points at $R$, modulo local systems. We use this to propose a general definition for microlocal perverse schobers supported on the open conormal to a germ of a hypersurface, and we conjecture that this is invariant under Radon transform. Here we make the key observation that while the analogous quotient of perverse schobers $\mathsf{2Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\mathsf{2Loc}(\mathbb{C})$ is a reasonable categorification, the resulting theory fails to be invariant under Radon transform. In fact, our proposed categorification can be recovered by correcting an instance of this failure in a universal manner. We prove Radon invariance when our hypersurface is the curve $y^m=x^n$ in $\mathbb{C}^2_{x,y}$. Along the way, we explain how our theory relates to Fourier transforms of perverse schobers, periodic SODs, and spherical monads.

</details>


### [230] [The Drinfeld associator and $A_\infty$ functors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19341)
*Nikita Markarian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文将半单霍洛诺米同构所联系的两个混合Hodge结构进行对比，计算其Hodge缺陷，并用逆Drinfeld associator与局部单值因子给出了普适的非交换级数表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $X=\mathbb P^1\setminus\{0,1,\infty\}$ 上，一元混合Hodge--Tate结构的变异 $\mathbb V$ 可诱导两个混合Hodge结构：一是在 $0$ 处以 $*$ 延拓、在 $\infty$ 处以 $!$ 延拓所得上同调；二是 $1$ 处的消没圈。二者由半单霍洛诺米同构在底层有理结构上等同且与权滤过相容，但未必保持Hodge滤过。作者旨在量化该不兼容性，即计算二者间的Hodge缺陷。

Method: 利用 [arXiv:2604.26357] 中构造的半单霍洛诺米同构，在有理结构层面识别上述两个混合Hodge结构。通过比较二者的Hodge滤过，定义并计算Hodge缺陷，并将其表示为普适的非交换级数：该级数由逆Drinfeld associator的一个特定分量与一个显式局部单值因子相乘给出。最后，用形式 $A_\infty$ 结构对Hodge缺陷的系数给出权框架Tate扩张的解释。

Result: 半单霍洛诺米同构与权滤过相容，但不保持Hodge滤过；由此产生的Hodge缺陷具有普适形式，其非交换级数等于逆Drinfeld associator的特定分量乘以显式局部单值因子。该缺陷的系数可在形式 $A_\infty$ 框架下用权框架Tate扩张刻画。

Conclusion: Hodge缺陷由逆Drinfeld associator与局部单值因子显式控制，并具有以权框架Tate扩张表述的形式 $A_\infty$ 解释，为混合Hodge--Tate变异的延拓上同调与消没圈之间的差异提供了精确的代数刻画。

Abstract: We compare two mixed Hodge structures attached to a unipotent variation $\mathbb V$ of mixed Hodge--Tate structures on $X=\mathbb P^1\setminus{0,1,\infty}$: the cohomology of its extension by $*$ at $0$ and by $!$ at $\infty$, and the vanishing cycles at $1$. The semi-holonomy isomorphism constructed in [arXiv:2604.26357] identifies these two structures on their underlying rational structures, compatibly with their weight filtrations, but need not respect their Hodge filtrations. We compute the resulting Hodge defect. Its universal noncommutative series is given by a specific component of the inverse Drinfeld associator multiplied by an explicit local monodromy factor. We conclude with a formal $A_\infty$ interpretation of the coefficients of the Hodge defect in terms of weight-framed Tate extensions.

</details>


### [231] [Balancedness of Normal Bundles of Rational Curves in Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19381)
*An Cao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在Grassmann流形$G(2,4)$、$G(2,5)$和$G(2,6)$上证明了一般有理曲线法丛平衡性的Coskun–Larson–Vogt猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在射影空间$\mathbb{P}^r$中，除特征2外，任意次数$d \geq r$的一般有理曲线其法丛是平衡的；然而Coskun、Larson和Vogt在arXiv:2404.08102中发现，该结论朴素推广到Grassmann流形上的有理曲线时不成立，并提出了关于法丛何时平衡的猜想。本文旨在验证该猜想在若干低维Grassmann流形上的正确性。

Method: 针对Grassmann流形$G(2,4)$、$G(2,5)$和$G(2,6)$，证明Coskun–Larson–Vogt提出的关于一般有理曲线法丛平衡性的猜想。

Result: 在$G(2,4)$、$G(2,5)$和$G(2,6)$上，所提出的猜想成立，即相应一般有理曲线的法丛满足平衡性条件。

Conclusion: 本文证实了Coskun–Larson–Vogt猜想在$G(2,4)$、$G(2,5)$、$G(2,6)$三个情形下成立，为将该平衡性理论从射影空间推广至Grassmann流形提供了部分支持。

Abstract: In projective space $\mathbb{P}^r$, the normal bundle of a general rational curve of any degree $d \geq r$ is balanced except over fields of characteristic 2. In arXiv:2404.08102, Coskun, Larson, and Vogt found counterexamples to the naive generalization of this statement to general rational curves in Grassmannians and proposed a conjecture for when the normal bundle is balanced. In this paper, we prove their conjecture for the Grassmannians $G(2, 4)$, $G(2, 5)$, and $G(2, 6)$.

</details>


### [232] [A Quadratically Enriched Pushforward for Deligne--Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19423)
*Candace Bethea,Kirsten Wickelgren*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用Grothendieck--Serre对偶，为任意域上光滑真Deligne--Mumford栈之间的真态射定义了二次enriched前推，并据此构造了相对定向向量丛的二次enriched欧拉类与欧拉数，进而为光滑真概型定义二次enriched基本类，并提出光滑、tame且相对定向的稳定映射模空间上二次enriched量子K-不变量的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意域（尤其是特征非零或非代数闭域）上，经典上同调与欧拉类理论往往丢失二次信息（如符号、定向），需要发展二次enriched（quadratically enriched）不变量理论来更精细地刻画几何对象。已有工作多局限于特定域或特定情形，本文旨在建立统一的、适用于Deligne--Mumford栈与一般域的前推与欧拉类框架，并延伸到量子K-不变量。

Method: 以Grothendieck--Serre对偶为核心工具，为光滑真Deligne--Mumford栈间的真态射构造二次enriched前推；以此为基础定义相对定向向量丛的二次enriched欧拉类与欧拉数；进一步为光滑真概型定义二次enriched基本类；最后将该框架应用于稳定映射模空间，定义二次enriched量子K-不变量。

Result: 成功定义了任意域上光滑真Deligne--Mumford栈间真态射的二次enriched前推；给出了相对定向向量丛的二次enriched欧拉类与欧拉数；构造了光滑真概型的二次enriched基本类；并在模空间光滑、tame且相对定向的条件下提出了二次enriched量子K-不变量的定义。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个基于Grothendieck--Serre对偶的二次enriched前推与欧拉类理论，统一处理任意域上的Deligne--Mumford栈与概型，并为二次enriched量子K-不变量提供了可行定义，为后续计算与镜像对称等相关研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We define a quadratically enriched pushforward for proper morphisms between smooth, proper Deligne--Mumford stacks over an arbitrary field using Grothendieck--Serre duality. We apply this pushforward to define quadratically enriched Euler classes and Euler numbers of relatively oriented vector bundles on smooth, proper Deligne--Mumford stacks. Finally, we define quadratically enriched fundamental classes for smooth, proper schemes over an arbitrary field, and we use these to propose a definition of quadratically enriched quantum $K$-invariants when the moduli space of stable maps is smooth, tame, and relatively oriented.

</details>


### [233] [Chow Vanishing and Motives of Cluster Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19744)
*Josephine Hlavinka*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety的积分Chow群与混合Hodge特定次数上同调群在正次数下消没，覆盖任意Lie型的braid variety与开Richardson variety，并由此推出Khovanov-Rozansky同调等消没与生成性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望统一处理一类重要簇（RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties）的上同调与Chow群消没现象，这类簇包含braid varieties与开Richardson varieties，进而关联Khovanov-Rozansky同调与Richardson/brick簇上同调的生成性质。

Method: 核心工具是对任意RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety构造分层，逐层为仿射空间与可变顶点更少的同类簇之积；结合Voevodsky motive理论，证明数域上mixed Tate簇的cycle class map同构到有理Borel-Moore同调的最低权部分，并证明此类簇具有mixed Tate且分裂的motive。

Result: 对RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety，CH^i与H^{2i,(i,i)}在i>0时消没；该结论适用于任意Lie型的braid varieties与开Richardson varieties；并给出正braid闭包的Khovanov-Rozansky同调消没定理，以及closed Richardson、projected Richardson与brick varieties上同调的生成性质。

Conclusion: 通过分层与motive方法，建立了RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties的消没定理并推广到多个几何与纽结同调场景，为相关簇的上同调生成问题提供统一框架。

Abstract: We prove that the integral Chow groups $CH^i$ and mixed Hodge degree $H^{2i, (i, i)}$ cohomology groups of really full rank (RFR) sink-recurrent cluster varieties vanish for $i > 0$. In particular this applies to braid varieties and open Richardson varieties in any Lie type. Our main tool is the construction of a stratification of any RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety $\mathcal{A}(Σ)$ into (affine spaces times) RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties of seeds with fewer mutable vertices than $Σ$.
  We employ the theory of Voevodsky motives, and towards this end we prove that the cycle class maps are isomorphisms onto the lowest-weight part of rational Borel-Moore homology for any mixed Tate variety over a number field. We then show that RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties have mixed Tate and, in fact, split motives. Finally, we use our results to deduce vanishing theorems about the Khovanov-Rozansky homology groups of closures of positive braids and generation properties of the cohomology of closed Richardson, projected Richardson, and brick varieties.

</details>


### [234] [The Bernstein-Sato Polynomial of the Hankel Determinant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19763)
*Xinyu Lin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文确定了 $m$ 阶一般对称 Hankel 矩阵行列式 $f_m$ 的整体 Bernstein--Sato 多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般对称 Hankel 矩阵行列式这一重要不变量的整体 Bernstein--Sato 多项式，该多项式编码了其奇点的单值性质，此前尚待确定。

Method: 结合对应割超曲面的 nearby cycles 与正定 Hankel 矩阵锥上的显式 Archimedean zeta 积分；用 Gauss--Radau 参数化将积分化为 Morris 积分并归结为 Gamma 函数之商，其原始极点由支集任意接近锥顶点的测试函数检测；用 Lichtin 定理对割层对数消解确定极点与 Bernstein--Sato 根之间的整数平移；用 nearby cycles 的相对 $D[s]$-格实现与相交复形的单性给出每个单值类中的唯一性与重数一；对矩阵规模的归纳来自割簇的局部乘积结构。

Result: 确定了 $f_m$ 的整体 Bernstein--Sato 多项式，其根由上述 zeta 积分的极点刻画，且在每个单值类中重数为 1。

Conclusion: 通过 nearby cycles、Archimedean zeta 积分、Lichtin 定理与 $D[s]$-格方法的结合，完整确定了对称 Hankel 行列式的 Bernstein--Sato 多项式，并证明其在各单值类中重数为一。

Abstract: Let $f_m(x_0,\ldots,x_{2m-2}) =\det(x_{i+j})_{0\leq i,j\leq m-1}$ be the determinant of the generic symmetric Hankel matrix of size $m$. We determine its global Bernstein--Sato polynomial. The proof combines the nearby cycles of the corresponding secant hypersurface with an explicit Archimedean zeta integral over the cone of positive definite Hankel matrices. A Gauss--Radau parametrization reduces this integral to a Morris integral and hence to a quotient of Gamma functions. Its primitive poles are detected by test functions supported arbitrarily close to the vertex of the cone. Lichtin's theorem, applied to a secant-stratum log resolution, fixes the integer shift between these poles and the roots of the Bernstein--Sato polynomial. A relative $D[s]$-lattice realization of nearby cycles, together with the simplicity of the corresponding intersection complexes, gives uniqueness and multiplicity one in every monodromy class. The induction in the matrix size follows from the local product structure of secant varieties.

</details>


### [235] [Bounds for Codimension-One Components of Zero Loci of Bernstein-Sato Ideals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19869)
*Wenzong Guo,Fanghan Xiang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究 Bernstein-Sato 理想 $B_F^{\mathbf a}$ 的零点轨迹，给出其上界 $c\leq L_E(\mathbf a)+(n-1-\delta_f)L_E(\mathbf 1)-k_E$，解决了 Budur–van der Veer–Van Werde 提出的关于任意元组的上界问题，并建立了局部指数比较的除子值表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Budur、van der Veer 与 Van Werde 提出了对任意元组（尤其 $r>1$）获得该类上界的问题。此前结果局限于特殊情形，缺少一般性的上界。本文旨在解决这一公开问题，并给出新的证明途径。

Method: 利用相对 D-模的局部化极大与极小扩张，给出 Bernstein-Sato 理想零点轨迹余维一分支为超平面 $L_E(\mathbf s)+k_E+c=0$（$c$ 为正整数）的新证明。通过比较 $Z(B_F^{\mathbf 1})$ 的对角切片与 $b_f$ 的根集，结合平移有限覆盖、对角特殊化与对数消解描述，证明两者具有相同的最小与最大点；再由 Saito 在公共最小点的根估计得到上界。最后给出局部指数比较的除子值表述。

Result: 证明了 $c\leq L_E(\mathbf a)+(n-1-\delta_f)L_E(\mathbf 1)-k_E$，其中 $\delta_f=\min\{n-1,\alpha_f\}$，$\alpha_f$ 为 $f$ 的最小指数，从而完整解决了任意元组情形下的上界问题。此外，恢复了通过单值 zeta 函数对单值支撑的检测以及多变量 A'Campo 公式。

Conclusion: 该工作以局部化扩张和对角特殊化给出了 Bernstein-Sato 理想零点轨迹结构的新证明与尖锐上界，统一并推广了此前结果；除子值形式的局部指数比较进一步联系了单值支撑检测与多变量 A'Campo 公式。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth complex affine variety of dimension $n$, and let $F=(f_1,\ldots,f_r)$ be a tuple of nonzero regular functions on $X$ such that $f:=\prod_{i=1}^r f_i$ is not invertible. We study the zero loci of the Bernstein-Sato ideals $B_F^{\mathbf a}$ for nonnegative integral shifts $\mathbf a$. For a fixed log resolution, every codimension-one irreducible component of $Z(B_F^{\mathbf a})$ is a hyperplane of the form $L_E(\mathbf s)+k_E+c=0$ with $c$ a positive integer. We give a new proof of this result using localized maximal and minimal extensions of relative D-modules. We also prove that $c\leq L_E(\mathbf a)+(n-1-δ_f)L_E(\mathbf 1)-k_E$, where $δ_f=\min\{n-1,α_f\}$ and $α_f$ is the minimal exponent of $f$. The problem of obtaining such an upper bound for arbitrary tuples (in particular, for $r>1$) was raised by Budur, van der Veer, and Van Werde, and the above inequality resolves it. To obtain the upper bound, we compare the diagonal slice of $Z(B_F^{\mathbf 1})$ with the root set of $b_f$. A finite covering by translates, combined with diagonal specialization and the log-resolution description, shows that these sets have the same least and greatest points. Saito's root estimate at their common least point then yields the upper bound. We further establish a divisor-valued formulation of the local index comparison, recovering the detection of monodromy support via monodromy zeta functions and the multivariable A'Campo formula.

</details>


### [236] [The unramified Grothendieck--Serre conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20015)
*Fei Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明：在非分歧正则局部环上，约化群概型的通用平凡 torsor 是平凡的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: torsor（主丛）的平凡性问题是代数几何与算术几何中的核心问题之一，常与局部-整体原则、Galois 上同调及约化群理论相关。该文关注非分歧正则局部环这一特殊但重要的基环情形，试图将已知的“通用平凡蕴含平凡”结果推广到更一般的情形。

Method: 在非分歧正则局部环上考察约化群概型 G 及其 torsor；利用“通用平凡”（generic triviality，即在分式域上平凡或仅在一般点处平凡）这一条件，通过局部环的几何性质与约化群的结构理论进行论证，推出该 torsor 本身 Zariski 局部平凡。

Result: 证明了定理：非分歧正则局部环上约化群概型的通用平凡 torsor 必为平凡 torsor。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了在非分歧正则局部环情形下通用平凡性与平凡性的一致性，为非分歧情形下的 torsor 平凡性提供了肯定回答，并可作为进一步研究更一般正则局部环或整体情形的基础。

Abstract: We prove that a generically trivial torsor under a reductive group scheme over an unramified regular local ring is trivial.

</details>


### [237] [Classification of Automorphism Groups of Smooth Cubic Threefolds and Fourfolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20403)
*Jie Fu,Shihao Wang,Zhiwei Zheng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文对光滑三次三维体和四维体的自同构群进行了分类，并给出分别含40个与156个连通族的分类结果，每族附有群作用与定义方程，方法上结合表示论（GAP）与格论（SageMath）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑三次超曲面的自同构群及其变形族分类是代数几何中的基本问题，已有结果不完整，作者希望给出三维体与四维体情形下的系统完整分类。

Method: 结合表示论（借助GAP计算）与格论（借助SageMath计算）进行分类，对每个连通族确定群作用并给出定义方程。

Result: 分类了光滑三次三维体与四维体的自同构群；给出分别含40个（三维体）与156个（四维体）具有特定自同构群作用的连通族，并对每族给出群作用与定义方程。

Conclusion: 实现了光滑三次三维体与四维体自同构群的完整分类，并提供了含群作用与定义方程的显式族列表，可作为后续研究的参考数据。

Abstract: We classify the automorphism groups of smooth cubic threefolds and fourfolds. We also show that there are $156$ (respectively, $40$) connected families of smooth cubic fourfolds (respectively, threefolds) with specified automorphism group action. For each family, the group action and defining equations are also given. The classification combines both representation theory (based on GAP) and lattice theory (based on SageMath).

</details>


### [238] [On Logarithmic Poisson and De Rham Cohomology Groups of a Class of Inhomogeneous Divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20119)
*Kamtila Kari,Dongho Joseph,Diekouam Fotso Luc Emery*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仿射平面上一类非齐次自由除子的对数Poisson上同调与对数de Rham上同调，显式构造了相应结构并计算了上同调，还给出了两套上同调理论之间的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非齐次自由除子（约化正规交叉除子的非齐次形变）上的对数Poisson与de Rham上同调，为这类除子提供显式上同调不变量，并揭示对数理论如何推广约化正规交叉除子的相应构造。

Method: 利用Saito意义下对数向量场模的显式基，构造对数Poisson结构，并在Lie-Rinehart代数框架下显式描述对数微分1-形式上的诱导Koszul括号；确定对数Poisson上链复形并计算其上同调；借助对数Spencer复形识别对数de Rham复形并计算其上同调；最后建立两套上同调理论之间的显式比较。

Result: 对所考虑的除子类，显式给出了对数Poisson上链复形及其上同调，以及对数de Rham复形及其上同调，得到显式上同调不变量。比较方面：对数Poisson与对数de Rham上同调在任意次数k≠1时自然同构；在次数1时，对数de Rham上同调群是对数Poisson上同调群的裂一维扩张。

Conclusion: 对数Poisson与de Rham理论将约化正规交叉除子的相应构造自然推广到所研究的非齐次自由除子类；两套对数上同调理论在k≠1时自然同构，仅在1次时相差一个裂一维扩张。

Abstract: We study logarithmic Poisson and de Rham cohomologies associated with a class of inhomogeneous free divisors in the affine plane. These divisors arise as inhomogeneous deformations of reduced normal crossing divisors and admit explicit bases for their modules of logarithmic vector fields in the sense of Saito. Using these bases, we construct the corresponding logarithmic Poisson structures and describe explicitly the induced Koszul bracket on logarithmic differential 1-forms within the framework of Lie-Rinehart algebras. We then determine the logarithmic Poisson cochain complex and compute its cohomology for the class under consideration. Furthermore, by means of the logarithmic Spencer complex, we identify the corresponding logarithmic de Rham complex and compute its cohomology. These computations provide explicit cohomological invariants for the class of inhomogeneous divisors considered and show how logarithmic Poisson and de Rham theories extend the corresponding constructions for reduced normal crossing divisors. In addition, we establish an explicit comparison between the logarithmic cohomological theories: they are naturally isomorphic in any degree $k \neq 1$, whereas in degree 1 the logarithmic de Rham cohomology group is a split one-dimensional extension of the logarithmic Poisson cohomology group.

</details>


### [239] [A Survey on Pham-Brieskorn Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20149)
*Michael Chitayat*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向研究生的综述，系统梳理 Pham–Brieskorn 簇的定义与各类性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pham–Brieskorn 簇是奇点理论与代数几何中的经典研究对象，但相关成果分散于不同文献，初学者缺少一份系统的入门性整理。

Method: 综述性方法：整理并归纳 Pham–Brieskorn 簇的构造定义及其已知性质，以研究生可理解的层次进行阐述。

Result: 汇总了 Pham–Brieskorn 簇在不同视角下的性质，形成一份便于入门阅读的综合材料。

Conclusion: Pham–Brieskorn 簇具有丰富而相互关联的性质，是奇点理论中适合研究生深入学习的重要对象。

Abstract: This is a survey on Pham-Brieskorn varieties and their various properties, targeted for graduate students.

</details>


### [240] [Uniform Rost nilpotence and birational motives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20228)
*David Kumallagov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究Chow动机在域扩张下的基变换理想 $I_E(M)$，给出了多类几何情形下的显式幂零界，并证明了有效的泛下降与定量提升结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Chow动机在域扩张下何时"消失"（即基变换后同态为零），是代数圈与动机理论中的核心问题；已有的Gille均匀界、Rosenschon--Sawant逐元素估计以及Kok--Zhou检测器结果分散且不统一，缺少理想层面的统一定量描述。

Method: 1) 定义基变换理想 $I_E(M)=\ker(\operatorname{End}_k(M)\to\operatorname{End}_E(M_E))$；2) 对multilinear Rost滤过引入加权局部块细化；3) 利用Kahn--Sujatha对纯双有理动机的描述；4) 将Kok--Zhou检测器从单个对应推广到整个基变换理想。

Result: 证明了显式幂零界；有效泛下降：若 $M$ 是 $h(X)(a)$ 的直和项且 $M_{k(X)}$ 具有一致Rost指数 $s$，则 $M$ 的指数为 $s(\dim X+1)$，从而给出 $2n-2$ 维扭Milnor超平面截面的整指数 $2n-1$。特征零下改进Gille的均匀界、将Rosenschon--Sawant估计升级为理想层面均匀界；得到从双有理动机到普通Chow动机的定量提升及三fold应用；并从临界精细未分歧上同调的均匀湮灭得到显式理想幂零界。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并强化了域扩张下Chow动机基变换理想的幂零性理论，在显式界、有效下降、双有理提升及检测器推广四方面取得进展。

Abstract: For a field extension $E/k$ and a Chow motive $M$, let $I_E(M)=\ker\bigl(\operatorname{End}_k(M)\longrightarrow \operatorname{End}_E(M_E)\bigr)$ be the base-change ideal. We establish explicit nilpotence bounds for these ideals in several geometric settings. We also prove effective generic descent: if $M$ is a summand of $h(X)(a)$ and $M_{k(X)}$ has uniform Rost exponent $s$, then $M$ has exponent $s(\dim X+1)$. This yields the integral exponent $2n-1$ for twisted Milnor hyperplane sections of dimension $2n-2$.
  In characteristic zero, a weighted local-block refinement of the multilinear Rost filtration improves the uniform bounds of Gille and upgrades the elementwise estimates of Rosenschon--Sawant to uniform bounds at the ideal level. Using the Kahn--Sujatha description of pure birational motives, we obtain quantitative lifting results from birational to ordinary Chow motives, with applications to threefolds, including varieties birational to toric models and varieties admitting a decomposition of the diagonal supported on a surface.
  Finally, we extend the Kok--Zhou detector from individual correspondences to entire base-change ideals, obtaining an explicit ideal nilpotence bound from uniform annihilation of the critical refined unramified cohomology groups.

</details>


### [241] [Cartier crystals and dual-constructible $p$-torsion complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20287)
*Jefferson Baudin,Kay Rülling*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在Noetherian且F-finite概型上，Cartier晶体上的不动点函子到Kato定义的平坦-相对完备site上的层范畴是全忠实的，其本质像为作者引入的对偶可构造层构成的子范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Cartier晶体与Kato平坦-相对完备site上层的比较，刻画不动点函子的像，为建立晶体理论与此类site上层的对应提供精确范畴刻画。

Method: 在Noetherian且F-finite概型上定义Cartier晶体上的不动点函子，将其映到Kato平坦-相对完备site上的层范畴；引入“对偶可构造层”子范畴以刻画其本质像。

Result: 不动点函子全忠实；本质像恰为对偶可构造层构成的子范畴。

Conclusion: Cartier晶体与Kato平坦-相对完备site上的对偶可构造层之间存在全忠实的等价刻画。

Abstract: We show that given a Noetherian and $F$-finite scheme, the fixed point functor on Cartier crystals is fully faithful to the category of sheaves on the flat and relatively perfect site defined by Kato, and its essential image consists of the subcategory of dual-constructible sheaves, which we introduce.

</details>


### [242] [An exponential Lojasiewicz inequality for semi-Pfaffian sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20361)
*Nicolai Vorobjov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文将Gabrielov关于Łojasiewicz不等式的版本推广到（不一定受限的）半Pfaff集，给出Pfaff函数在半Pfaff集X上、相对于其零点集以及区域边界上零点的增长速率刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Łojasiewicz不等式及其多数变体只刻画函数在其定义域内部的零点附近的增长速率，而Gabrielov的版本本质上是一维的，无法处理更一般的高维情形与边界零点。因此作者希望突破维数限制，给出多维、且适用于不一定受限的半Pfaff集的推广形式。

Method: 证明Gabrielov所给Łojasiewicz不等式版本的一个推广：在半Pfaff集X上，对Pfaff函数刻画其不仅相对于X定义域内零点集邻域、而且相对于定义域边界上零点邻域的增长速率；将Gabrielov本质上一维的结果提升到多维情形。

Result: 证明了适用于不一定受限的半Pfaff集的多维Łojasiewicz不等式推广，统一覆盖函数零点集（内部）与定义域边界零点两处的增长速率估计。

Conclusion: Gabrielov的Łojasiewicz不等式版本可以从一维情形推广到多维半Pfaff集的一般框架，从而在该设定下同时给出内部零点与边界零点附近的增长速率界。

Abstract: We prove a generalization of the version, due to A. Gabrielov, of the Łojasiewicz inequality for not necessarily restricted semi-Pfaffian sets. Unlike the most variants of the Łojasiewicz inequality, it describes the rate of growth of a Pfaffian function on a semi-Pfaffian set $X$ not only in a neighbourhood of its zero set in the domain of $X$ but also in a neighbourhood of zeroes on the boundary of the domain. Gabrielov's version is essentially one-dimensional, we generalize it to a multidimensional case.

</details>


### [243] [On the ACC for Minimal Log Discrepancies for Bounded Generalized Sub-Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20515)
*Weichung Chen,Keng-Hung Steven Lin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了ACC猜想（关于极小对数不变量）在两类情形下成立：任意维数的有界广义子对，以及带任意边界的有界三维簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACC猜想（极小对数不变量的上升链条件）是双有理几何中极小模型纲领的核心问题之一，此前仅在有限情形下被验证。本文旨在将其推广到更一般的设定。

Method: 通过对"有界广义子对"（bounded generalized sub-pairs）以及"有界三维簇带任意边界"两类对象进行分析，运用极小模型纲领与对对数不变量（log discrepancies）的估计技术进行证明。

Result: ACC猜想在以下两种情形下成立：（1）任意维数的有界广义子对；（2）带任意边界的有界三维簇。

Conclusion: 该结果显著拓宽了ACC猜想的已知成立范围，涵盖任意维数的广义子对及三维情形下的任意边界，为后续完全解决ACC猜想提供了重要进展。

Abstract: We show that the ACC conjecture for minimal log discrepancies holds for both bounded generalized sub-pairs in arbitrary dimension and bounded threefolds with arbitrary boundaries.

</details>


### [244] [Conductor-Discriminant Inequality for Tamely Ramified Cyclic Covers I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20553)
*Andrew Obus,Padmavathi Srinivasan,Connor Stewart*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了定义在离散赋值域上的 $\mathbb{Z}/n$-覆盖的导子-判别式不等式，将超椭圆曲线的结果推广到一般循环覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前第一、二作者在超椭圆曲线上建立了导子-判别式不等式，Ogg、Saito、Liu 及第二作者亦有相关工作，而 Kohls 给出了 Jacobian 导子指数的上界。本文旨在将这些结果统一推广到更一般的 $\mathbb{Z}/n$-覆盖情形。

Method: 设 $K$ 为离散赋值域，其赋值环 $\mathcal{O}_K$ 为 excellent，剩余域完美且特征不整除 $n$。考虑由 $y^n=f(x)$ 给出、且 $n\mid\deg(f)$ 的曲线 $X$，取其极小正规模型 $\mathcal{X}$，通过 Artin 导子与判别式（包括 rad(f) 的判别式）的计算来证明不等式。

Result: 当曲线 $X$ 由 $y^n=f(x)$（$f\in\mathcal{O}_K[x]$，$n\mid\deg(f)$）给出、且 $\mathcal{X}$ 为其在 $\mathcal{O}_K$ 上的极小正规模型时，$\mathcal{X}$ 的 Artin 导子的负值被 $(n-1)v_K(\mathrm{disc}(\mathrm{rad}(f)))$ 上界控制。当 $f$ 为首一多项式时，该结果强化了 Kohls 关于此类曲线 Jacobian 导子指数的上界。

Conclusion: 该工作将超椭圆曲线的导子-判别式不等式直接推广到 $\mathbb{Z}/n$-覆盖，统一并强化了 Ogg、Saito、Liu、Kohls 等的相关结果，模去第三作者工作中的某些计算。

Abstract: We prove conductor-discriminant inequalities for all $\mathbb{Z}/n$-covers of $\mathbb{P}^1$ defined over discretely valued fields $K$ with excellent valuation ring $\mathcal{O}_K$ and perfect residue field of characteristic not dividing $n$, modulo some calculations appearing in work of the third author. Specifically, when such a curve $X$ is given by $y^n = f(x)$ with $f(x) \in\mathcal{O}_K[x]$ and $n\mid\text{deg}(f)$, and if $\mathcal{X}$ is its minimal regular model over $\mathcal{O}_K$, then the negative of the Artin conductor of $\mathcal{X}$ is bounded above by $(n-1)v_K(\text{disc}(\text{rad}(f)))$. This is a direct generalization of previous work of the first two authors on hyperelliptic curves, which in turn generalized work of Ogg, Saito, Liu, and the second author. When $f$ is monic, this strengthens a result of Kohls stating that the conductor exponent of the Jacobian of such a curve is bounded above by $(n-1)v_K(\text{disc}(\text{rad}(f)))$.

</details>


### [245] [The Hurwitz existence problem in prime degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20572)
*Jijian Song,Hailin Wen,Zebao Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了球面上任意度数为素数 $p$ 的相容分支数据均可由连通分支覆盖实现，并在多个相关不变量上得到正性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究素数度数分支覆盖的可实现性问题，即球面上给定分支数据是否总能由连通分支覆盖实现，并由此推导相关 Hurwitz 势与 Gromov–Witten 不变量的正性。

Method: 三点情形在剩余特征 $p$ 下构造；利用 Henrio 矩定理获得互异点矩解，构造每个给定分拆所需的特殊原始尾部，并针对正源亏格构造新尾部；通过对数形变数据将这些尾部连接，嵌入 $S_p$ 中含公共 $p$ 阶正规子群的子群；再用 Wewers 提升定理得到特征零下的三点 Galois 覆盖，对点稳定子取商得到度 $p$ 且具有指定三分支剖面的覆盖；最后用 Edmonds–Kulkarni–Stong 的融合与实现结果推广到任意分支值个数。

Result: 任意度数为 $p$ 的相容分支数据均可由连通分支覆盖实现；连通素数度 Hurwitz 势在 Riemann–Hurwitz 轨迹上满支撑；$\mathbf P^1$ 的相应连通相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量均非零；满足维数约束下，偶数完成循环阶至多 $p$ 的两相对点非连通扇区严格为正；连通对换扇区严格为正。

Conclusion: 该文完整解决了素数度数分支覆盖的可实现性问题，并给出了 Hurwitz 势满支撑及若干相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量正性的推论。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime. We prove that every compatible branch datum of degree $p$ over the sphere is realizable by a connected branched cover. The three-point case is constructed in residue characteristic $p$. Henrio's moment theorem supplies the distinct-point moment solutions from which we construct a special primitive tail for each prescribed partition; a second application underlies the new tail required by a positive source genus. These tails are joined by a logarithmic deformation datum and embedded in one subgroup of $S_p$ containing a common regular subgroup of order $p$. Wewers's lifting theorem produces a three-point Galois cover in characteristic zero. The quotient by a point stabilizer has degree $p$ and the prescribed three ramification profiles. The fusion and realization results of Edmonds--Kulkarni--Stong then give the assertion for an arbitrary number of branch values. As consequences, the connected prime-degree Hurwitz potential has full support on the Riemann--Hurwitz locus, every corresponding connected relative Gromov--Witten invariant of $\mathbf P^1$ is nonzero, the two-relative-point disconnected sector with even completed-cycle orders at most $p$ is strictly positive subject to the dimension constraint, and the connected transposition sector is strictly positive.

</details>


### [246] [Conductor-Discriminant Inequality for Tamely Ramified Cyclic Covers II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20585)
*Connor Stewart*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是前作的配套技术论文，围绕导子-判别式贡献 cdc(y) 给出必要技术结果：证明 cdc(y) 在多数情况下非负，刻画其负值出现的强限制条件，并说明负值时归一化局部环的谱为有理二重点；同时证明相关量 cedc(y) 的非负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前作（与 Obus、Srinivasan 合作）中，作者定义了分支因子重数 2 点 y 的导子-判别式贡献 cdc(y)，并在此假设下证明了 X 的导子-判别式不等式，推广了 Ogg、Saito、Liu、Srinivasan 及 Obus–Srinivasan 的结果。但该不等式依赖于若干关于 cdc(y) 的关键结果尚未证明，因此需要配套论文补齐这些技术性证明。

Method: 设 K 为 Hensel 离散赋值域，整数环 O_K 优秀，剩余域 k 代数闭。考虑循环覆盖 X→P^1_K，次数 n 与 k 的特征互素。对正则 O_K-模型 Y 在 K(X) 中归一化的分支因子的重数 2 点 y，定义 cdc(y)，并通过局部分析（完全局部环、归一化、分支因子结构）研究其符号与几何性质。

Result: 证明 cdc(y) 非负，除非 n 与分支因子在 y 处的结构满足高度限制性条件；当 cdc(y) 为负时，归一化后在 y 上任意点的完全局部环的谱为有理二重点。此外证明相关量 cedc(y)（导子指数-判别式贡献）非负，该量用于在前作中给出 Kohls 结果的新证明。

Conclusion: 本文补全了前作中关于 cdc(y) 的关键技术结果，确立了 cdc(y) 的非负性及其负值情况下的有理二重点刻画，并证明 cedc(y) 非负，从而支撑导子-判别式不等式的证明及 Kohls 结果的新证。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a Henselian discretely valued field with excellent ring of integers $\mathcal{O}_K$ and algebraically closed residue field $k$. Let $X\to\mathbb{P}^1_K$ be a cyclic cover of degree $n$ prime to the characteristic of $k$. In joint work with Obus and Srinivasan, we define an integer called the conductor-discriminant contribution $\text{cdc}(y)$ associated to a multiplicity $2$ point $y$ of the branch divisor of the normalization in $K(X)$ of a regular $\mathcal{O}_K$-model $\mathcal{Y}$ of $\mathbb{P}^1_{K}$, and modulo several key results about $\text{cdc}(y)$, we prove a conductor-discriminant inequality for $X$, extending previous work of Ogg, Saito, Liu, Srinivasan, and Obus--Srinivasan. In this companion paper, we supply the necessary technical results for $\text{cdc}(y)$. In particular, we show $\text{cdc}(y)$ is non-negative except under highly restrictive conditions on $n$ and the structure of the branch divisor at $y$. Moreover, if $\text{cdc}(y)$ is negative, we show the spectrum of the complete local ring of any point lying over $y$ under the normalization of $\mathcal{Y}$ in $K(X)$ is a rational double point. Along the way, we show the non-negativity of a related quantity, the conductor exponent-discriminant contribution $\text{cedc}(y)$, which is used in our joint work with Obus and Srinivasan to give a new proof of a result of Kohls.

</details>


### [247] [The Last Picard Rank 1 Double-Mirror Calabi-Yau Pair?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20704)
*Michał Kapustka,Marco Rampazzo,Prajwal Samal*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究两族Picard秩为1的Calabi-Yau三维簇（$G(2,6)$中33次簇族$\mathcal{X}$与$\mathbb{P}^8$中算术Gorenstein的21次簇族$\mathcal{Y}$），建立二者的几何对应与对偶关系，并通过数学GLSM证明它们导出等价，从而验证了Miura与Gerhardus-Jockers的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两族Calabi-Yau三维簇此前在互不相关的数学文献中独立出现，其潜在关联未被认识；同时Miura基于镜像模空间研究预测$\mathcal{X}$存在非平凡Fourier-Mukai搭档，Gerhardus-Jockers亦从物理角度提出同一猜想。作者意在建立两族的几何联系并验证这一猜想。

Method: 建立两族一般成员之间的自然几何对应；对相应簇对验证经典对偶性；构造数学GLSM（gauged linear sigma model）以联系其几何，并据此证明导出等价；此外从$\mathcal{Y}$族出发推测得到同一镜像模空间。

Result: 证明对应簇对满足经典对偶性；借助GLSM证明两族相应三维簇导出等价，确认了Miura的猜想（即$\mathcal{X}$成员存在非平凡Fourier-Mukai搭档），并从$\mathcal{Y}$出发推测性地得到相同的镜像模空间；该簇对构成Picard秩为1、非双有理双镜像Calabi-Yau三维簇形变族短名单中的新成员。

Conclusion: 该簇对很可能是Picard秩为1的非双有理双镜像Calabi-Yau三维簇形变族小列表中的最后一次新增，为Calabi-Yau几何与镜像对称研究提供了新的重要实例。

Abstract: We consider a certain pair of families of Picard rank 1 Calabi-Yau threefolds, that have appeared earlier in mathematical literature in unrelated contexts: the family $\mathcal{X}$ of degree 33 threefolds in $G(2,6)$ (constructed by Inoue-Ito-Miura) and the family $\mathcal{Y}$ of arithmetically Gorenstein degree 21 threefolds in $\mathbb{P}^8$ (constructed by Schenck-Stillman-Yuan). After establishing a natural geometric correspondence between their general members, we go on to show that any pair of corresponding threefolds in these families satisfy certain classical dualities. We moreover discover that their geometries may be related by a mathematical gauged linear sigma model, using which we prove that they are derived equivalent. This settles a conjecture of Miura, who predicted the existence of non-trivial Fourier-Mukai partners to members of $\mathcal{X}$, based on a study of its mirror moduli. This conjecture was also formulated later by Gerhardus-Jockers, in a physical context. In fact, starting from the other family $\mathcal{Y}$, we conjecturally arrive at the same mirror moduli. Thus, such a pair is a new, and quite possibly the last, addition to the small list of deformation families of non-birational, double-mirror Calabi-Yau threefolds having Picard rank 1.

</details>


### [248] [Connecting families of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20716)
*Nathan Chen,Robert Lazarsfeld,Federico Moretti*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究光滑射影簇 X 上经过大量一般点的曲线族的亏格与 gonality（gonality），在非负 Kodaira 维数情形给出关于点数与维数的线性下界，并刻画 gonality 极限值；同时讨论高维连接子簇的标准体积线性界与超曲面渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Kollár–Miyaoka–Mori 定理（有理连通簇上任意有限点集落在一条有理曲线上）启发，自然要问：对任意光滑射影簇 X，经过其许多一般点的曲线族有何性质？当 X 具有非负 Kodaira 维数时，希望将 Arapura–Archava 的定理推广，并理解连接族 gonality 随点数增长的行为。

Method: 设 X 为光滑射影簇且 Kodaira 维数非负；对连接给定数量一般点的曲线族，建立亏格关于点数与 dim X 的尖锐线性下界；研究连接族最小可能 gonality 随点数增加的稳定性，并用支配 X、且为有理连通簇的 generically finite 覆盖的簇来刻画其极限值；对超曲面在大次数情形分析这些不变量的联合渐近；最后将方法推广到高维连接子簇，证明其标准体积的线性界。

Result: 在非负 Kodaira 维数情形，得到连接族亏格的尖锐线性下界，推广 Arapura–Archava 定理；证明连接族的最小 gonality 随点数增长最终稳定，并给出极限值的刻画；对超曲面确定最小连接亏格在点数与大次数变化下的联合渐近行为；对高维连接子簇证明标准体积的线性上界，并给出超曲面的渐近结果。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了一般光滑射影簇上连接多点曲线族的亏格与 gonality 随点数的增长规律，给出尖锐线性界与稳定极限的几何解释，并将结论扩展至高维连接子簇的标准体积，超曲面为典型应用示例。

Abstract: A theorem of Kollár, Miyaoka, and Mori states that on a rationally connected variety, any finite collection of points lies on a rational curve. Motivated by this, it is natural to ask what one can say about families of curves passing through many general points of an arbitrary smooth projective variety X. When X has nonnegative Kodaira dimension, we establish a sharp linear lower bound for the genus of such curves in terms of the number of points and the dimension of X, generalizing a theorem of Arapura and Archava. By contrast, the least possible gonality of a connecting family eventually stabilizes as the number of points grows. We characterize its limiting value in terms of varieties dominating X that are generically finite covers of rationally connected varieties. As an illustration, we study these invariants for hypersurfaces of large degree, determining in particular the joint asymptotic behavior of the minimal connecting genus as the number of points and the degree vary. Finally, we briefly consider higher-dimensional connecting subvarieties, proving linear bounds for their canonical volumes and computing asymptotic results for hypersurfaces.

</details>


### [249] [Morphism spaces on low degree hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20783)
*Hrishabh Mishra*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究参数化固定次数态射 $\mathbb P^r\to X$ 的模空间：当 $n> 2^d(d-1)\binom{de+r-1}{r-1}$ 时，$\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r, X)$ 是不可约的且维数符合预期，将 Browning-Yamagishi 的 $r=2$ 结果推广到任意 $r>2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画低次光滑超曲面 $X$ 上从射影空间 $\mathbb P^r$ 出发的固定次数态射的模空间结构，是丢番图几何与代数几何交叉中的重要问题；已有结果仅覆盖 $r=2$ 的情形，需要向高维推广。

Method: 采用 multiblock Weyl differencing（多重块 Weyl 差分）方法，在特征为零或大于 $d$ 的代数闭域上研究 $\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r, X)$，并通过该解析数论式技术与几何模空间论证结合给出不可约性与维数估计。

Result: 设 $n\geq 2, e\geq 1$，$X\subset \mathbb P^{n-1}$ 为光滑 $d$ 次超曲面（$d\geq 2$），若 $n> 2^d(d-1)\binom{de+r-1}{r-1}$，则 $\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r, X)$ 不可约且具有预期维数。

Conclusion: 该结果把 Browning-Yamagishi 关于 $r=2$ 的结论推广至任意 $r>2$，表明在维数 $n$ 充分大（相对 $d,e,r$ 呈指数/多项式界）时态射模空间具有良好几何性质。

Abstract: For $r> 2$, we study the moduli space parameterising fixed degree morphisms $\mathbb P^r\to X$ where $X$ is a smooth hypersurface of low degree. More precisely, we prove the following result: let $n\geq 2, e\geq 1$ and $X\subset \mathbb P^{n-1}$ a smooth degree $d\geq 2$ hypersurface over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero or greater than $d$, then $\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r, X)$ is irreducible of the expected dimension if
  \[
  n> 2^d(d-1)\binom{de+r-1}{r-1}.
  \]
  Our result extends the result of Browning-Yamagishi for $r=2$ via multiblock Weyl differencing.

</details>
